**21:45** — Готовлю chroot в образе (qemu, bind-mount, DNS) и проверяю apk внутри

```sh
M=/mnt/pmos-lomiri; cat /proc/sys/fs/binfmt_misc/qemu-aarch64 | head -4; ls -l $M/etc/resolv.conf $M/etc/apk/repositories 2>&1 | awk '{print $1,$9,$10,$11}'; sudo cp /usr/bin/qemu-aarch64-static $M/usr/bin/qemu-aarch64-static; for d in proc sys dev dev/pts; do sudo mount --bind /$d $M/$d 2>&1 | head -1; done; sudo cp -L $M/etc/resolv.conf /tmp/resolv.conf.image-orig 2>/dev/null; sudo rm -f $M/etc/resolv.conf; printf 'nameserver 1.1.1.1\nnameserver 8.8.8.8\n' | sudo tee $M/etc/resolv.conf >/dev/null; sudo chroot $M /bin/sh -c 'apk --version; cat /etc/apk/repositories; apk info -e postmarketos-ui-plasma-mobile lomiri 2>&1 | head -3; apk update 2>&1 | tail -2'
```

**21:47** — Создаю службу запуска Lomiri и переключатель интерфейса, отключаю лишние службы Lomiri

```sh
M=/mnt/pmos-lomiri; sudo rm -f $M/etc/systemd/system/default.target.wants/hfd-service.service $M/etc/systemd/system/graphical.target.wants/sensorfwd.service; sudo tee $M/usr/lib/systemd/system/m52xq-lomiri.service >/dev/null <<'EOF'
[Unit]
Description=M52XQ: Lomiri (Wayland, Mir) instead of Plasma Mobile on the same tty
Documentation=https://lomiri.com/

# replaces the getty and the Plasma Mobile session (only one user interface runs at a time)
Conflicts=getty@tty1.service plasma-mobile.service
After=getty@tty1.service
After=rc-local.service plymouth-quit-wait.service systemd-user-sessions.service
OnFailure=getty@tty1.service

Wants=dbus.socket
After=dbus.socket
After=session-c1.scope
Before=graphical.target

# the background services of Lomiri run only together with it (not enabled on their own: they fight Plasma for the sensors)
Wants=sensorfwd.service hfd-service.service

ConditionPathExists=/dev/tty0

[Service]
Environment=LANG=C.UTF-8
Environment=XDG_SEAT=seat0
Environment=XDG_VTNR=tty1
ExecStart=/usr/bin/dbus-run-session /usr/bin/start_lomiri
TimeoutStartSec=60
User=1000
PAMName=login
WorkingDirectory=~
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=5s

TTYPath=/dev/tty1
TTYReset=yes
TTYVHangup=yes
TTYVTDisallocate=yes
StandardInput=tty-fail
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal
UtmpIdentifier=tty1
UtmpMode=user

# not [Install]ed: m52xq-ui-switch lomiri points display-manager.service at it
EOF
sudo mkdir -p $M/usr/local/sbin; sudo tee $M/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-ui-switch >/dev/null <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# M52XQ: which user interface starts on this system: plasma (Plasma Mobile, default) or lomiri (Lomiri).
#   m52xq-ui-switch plasma|lomiri   - makes it the default (display-manager.service) and switches to it now (the session restarts)
#   m52xq-ui-switch status          - which one is the default and which one runs
D=/etc/systemd/system/display-manager.service
case $1 in
plasma | lomiri)
	[ "$1" = plasma ] && U=plasma-mobile.service || U=m52xq-lomiri.service
	O=plasma-mobile.service; [ "$1" = plasma ] && O=m52xq-lomiri.service
	ln -sf /usr/lib/systemd/system/$U $D
	systemctl daemon-reload
	echo "default interface: $1; switching now"
	# run detached: the call may come from inside the session that is being stopped
	systemd-run --on-active=2 --unit=m52xq-ui-switch-now sh -c "systemctl stop $O; systemctl reset-failed $U 2>/dev/null; systemctl start $U" >/dev/null
# … ещё 10 строк
```

```sh
M=/mnt/pmos-lomiri; sudo rm -f $M/etc/resolv.conf; sudo ln -s ../run/systemd/resolve/stub-resolv.conf $M/etc/resolv.conf; ls -l $M/etc/resolv.conf | awk '{print $9,$10,$11}'; sudo rm -f $M/usr/bin/qemu-aarch64-static; for d in dev/pts dev sys proc; do sudo umount $M/$d 2>&1 | head -1; done; mount | grep -c "pmos-lomiri/\(proc\|sys\|dev\)"; sudo df -h $M | tail -1; sudo umount $M && echo "образ размонтирован"; cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002; e2fsck -fn rootfs.img 2>&1 | tail -2; ls -la rootfs.img; md5sum rootfs.img | tee rootfs.img.md5
```

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002; O="-o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o ServerAliveInterval=15"; H=192.168.1.167; sshpass -p <пароль> ssh $O root@172.16.42.1 true 2>/dev/null && H=172.16.42.1; echo "канал: $H"; sshpass -p <пароль> ssh $O root@$H 'ls -l /mnt/userdata/rootfs.img* 2>&1 | awk "{print \$5,\$9}"; df -h /mnt/userdata | tail -1'; time (gzip -1 -c rootfs.img | sshpass -p <пароль> ssh $O root@$H 'systemd-inhibit --what=sleep:idle --who=m52xq-upload --why="rootfs.img (pmOS+Lomiri) to userdata" sh -c "gunzip > /mnt/userdata/rootfs.img.part && sync && mv /mnt/userdata/rootfs.img.part /mnt/userdata/rootfs.img" && echo upload-done')
```

**21:47** — Дополняю README в убунтутач24 результатом сборки образа Lomiri

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24; python3 - <<'EOF'
p = 'README.md'
s = open(p).read()
old = "### Состояние (обновляется)\n* 2026-10-24: образ 8 ГиБ создан, идёт копирование корня с телефона. Дальше — chroot-установка Lomiri (см. раздел «Журнал» ниже).\n"
assert old in s
new = """### Что получилось (2026-10-24 ~00:50)
* `002/rootfs.img` — ext4 8 ГиБ, метка `pmOS_root`, **md5 `94d170976e4e1e95332ae72bd45c1663`** (`002/rootfs.img.md5`), `e2fsck` чисто (133070 inodes, 1311803/2097152 блоков, занято 4,8 ГБ, свободно 2,6 ГБ).
  Внутри: копия работающей системы телефона (Plasma Mobile и все наши патчи/службы/данные телефона) **плюс Lomiri** (`postmarketos-ui-lomiri`, 1696 пакетов в базе, `lomiri`, `mir`,
  `lomiri-system-settings`, `repowerd`, `sensorfw`, `hfd-service` …). Журнал установки: `002/apk-add-lomiri.log`.
* Установка завершилась кодом 1 из-за ОДНОЙ несущественной ошибки: триггер `postmarketos-mkinitfs` — `No kernel found in /boot` (в образе нет ядра, загрузчик свой). Все пакеты установлены.
  Вторая особенность: пресет не смог переопределить `display-manager.service` — он остался ссылкой на `plasma-mobile.service` (то, что нужно: по умолчанию Plasma).
* Установка Lomiri включила в системе `hfd-service` (default.target) и `sensorfwd` (graphical.target): они мешали бы Plasma (датчики, питание) — **эти две ссылки удалены**,
  вместо них служба запуска Lomiri сама подтягивает их (`Wants=`).
* Добавлено в образ: `/usr/lib/systemd/system/m52xq-lomiri.service` (запуск Lomiri на tty1 по образцу `plasma-mobile.service`: `User=1000`, `PAMName=login`,
  `ExecStart=/usr/bin/dbus-run-session /usr/bin/start_lomiri`, `Conflicts=getty@tty1.service plasma-mobile.service`, не включена) и `/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-ui-switch`
  (`plasma` | `lomiri` | `status`: ставит `display-manager.service` на нужную службу и переключается через `systemd-run`, чтобы вызов из самого сеанса не убивал себя).
* После установки приведено в порядок: `resolv.conf` снова ссылка `../run/systemd/resolve/stub-resolv.conf`, `qemu-aarch64-static` удалён из образа, bind-mount-ы сняты.

### Как проверить (после заливки в `/data/rootfs.img`)
1. Перезагрузить телефон: загрузчик найдёт `/data/rootfs.img` (раздел 0) и поднимет эту систему. Должна запуститься **Plasma Mobile, как обычно** (интерфейс по умолчанию — Plasma).
2. По ssh (`172.16.42.1` или Wi-Fi): `m52xq-ui-switch status`, затем `m52xq-ui-switch lomiri` — перейти на Lomiri; `m52xq-ui-switch plasma` — вернуться. Откат всего эксперимента: убрать `/data/rootfs.img` в TWRP.
3. Если Lomiri не стартует: `journalctl -u m52xq-lomiri -b`, `systemctl status m52xq-lomiri`; при падении `OnFailure=getty@tty1` вернёт консоль (ssh остаётся).

### Состояние (обновляется)
* 2026-10-24 00:50: образ собран, идёт заливка в `/data/rootfs.img` на телефоне (перезагрузка не делалась).
"""
s = s.replace(old, new)
s = s.replace("* **2026-10-24 ~21:5x** — создан `002/rootfs.img`, начато копирование корня телефона.\n", "* **2026-10-24 ~21:5x** — создан `002/rootfs.img`, начато копирование корня телефона (2 мин 13 с, 4,4 ГБ, 0 ошибок cpio).\n* **2026-10-24 ~22:3x** — в образ через chroot (qemu-aarch64) установлена Lomiri (1 некритичная ошибка триггера mkinitfs), добавлены `m52xq-lomiri.service`, `m52xq-ui-switch`; e2fsck чисто, md5 `94d17097…`; залит на телефон в `/data/rootfs.img`.\n")
open(p, 'w').write(s)
EOF
grep -c "94d170976e4e" README.md
```

**21:54** — Готовлю файлы кнопки выбора интерфейса (служба, polkit, окно QML, запуск, ярлык)

```sh
B=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002/extra-files; mkdir -p $B/etc/systemd/system $B/etc/polkit-1/rules.d $B/usr/local/bin $B/usr/local/share/m52xq $B/usr/share/applications
cat > $B/etc/systemd/system/m52xq-ui-switch@.service <<'EOF'
[Unit]
Description=M52XQ: switch the user interface to %i (plasma or lomiri)

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-ui-switch %i
EOF
cat > $B/etc/polkit-1/rules.d/50-m52xq-ui-switch.rules <<'EOF'
// M52XQ: the "Interface" button (Plasma / Lomiri) may start m52xq-ui-switch@plasma|lomiri without a password (active local user)
polkit.addRule(function(action, subject) {
	if (action.id == "org.freedesktop.systemd1.manage-units" &&
	    action.lookup("verb") == "start" &&
	    /^m52xq-ui-switch@(plasma|lomiri)\.service$/.test(action.lookup("unit")) &&
	    subject.local && subject.active)
		return polkit.Result.YES;
});
EOF
cat > $B/usr/local/bin/m52xq-ui-switch-menu <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# M52XQ: "Interface" button: a window with two big buttons, Plasma Mobile / Lomiri (the current one is highlighted).
# The choice restarts the session: m52xq-ui-switch@<plasma|lomiri>.service (root, allowed for the active user by
# /etc/polkit-1/rules.d/50-m52xq-ui-switch.rules) makes it the default (display-manager.service) and switches to it.
cur=plasma
systemctl is-active -q m52xq-lomiri.service && cur=lomiri
qml=${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/tmp}/m52xq-ui-switch-menu.qml
sed "s/@CURRENT@/$cur/" /usr/local/share/m52xq/ui-switch-menu.qml > "$qml"
choice=$(/usr/lib/qt6/bin/qmlscene "$qml" 2>&1 | sed -n 's/.*m52xq-ui-choice: //p' | tail -n 1)
rm -f "$qml"
case $choice in
plasma | lomiri)
	[ "$choice" = "$cur" ] || systemctl start "m52xq-ui-switch@$choice.service"
	;;
esac
exit 0
EOF
cat > $B/usr/local/share/m52xq/ui-switch-menu.qml <<'EOF'
// M52XQ: "Interface" window (qmlscene), started by m52xq-ui-switch-menu: @CURRENT@ = plasma | lomiri.
// Two big buttons; a choice is printed as "m52xq-ui-choice: <plasma|lomiri>" (the script does the switch) and the window closes.
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls
import QtQuick.Layouts
import QtQuick.Window

Window {
	id: win
	visible: true
	visibility: Window.FullScreen
	title: "Interface"
	color: "#101418"

	readonly property string current: "@CURRENT@"
	readonly property real u: Math.min(width, height) / 20

	function choose(a) {
		console.log("m52xq-ui-choice: " + a)
		Qt.quit()
	}

# … ещё 68 строк
```

**22:03** — Записываю в README итог первого запуска Lomiri и найденные исправления

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24; python3 - <<'EOF'
p = 'README.md'
s = open(p).read()
anchor = "### Как проверить (после заливки в `/data/rootfs.img`)"
add = """### Первый запуск Lomiri (2026-10-24 ~01:00–01:05, нажатие «Lomiri» в кнопке выбора интерфейса)
Телефон НЕ перезагружался: переключился только сеанс. Что нашлось и исправлено прямо на телефоне (в образе `/data/rootfs.img`):
1. `m52xq-lomiri.service` падала с `status=217/USER`, «Failed to determine credentials for user '1000': Unknown user»: в моей копии службы стояло `User=1000`, а у Plasma пользователя подменяет
   генератор systemd (`/run/systemd/generator/plasma-mobile.service.d/10-user.conf`: `User=10000`, `Group=10000`; на телефоне `user` = uid 10000). **Исправлено:** в службе `User=10000`, `Group=10000`
   (копия в `002/extra-files/usr/lib/systemd/system/m52xq-lomiri.service`). Пока служба падала, `OnFailure=getty@tty1` поднимал консоль на экране.
2. Дальше Lomiri запустилась, графика заработала (Mir: gbm-kms, `/dev/dri/card1` msm_dpu, экран `DSI-1` 1080×2400, рендеринг подключён, датчик ориентации читается), но экран был БЕЛЫМ:
   `Components/ScreenshotEditor.qml: module "Lomiri.Components.Extras" is not installed` → оболочка не загрузилась. **Исправлено:** `apk add lomiri-ui-extras-qt5` (в базу образа; после этого экран стал серым —
   оболочка грузится, остались предупреждения «Cannot read property … of null» при старте и `QPainter: Painter not active`).
3. Не было `/usr/share/backgrounds/lomiri-default-background.png` (каталога `backgrounds` нет) → создан как копия `/usr/share/wallpapers/postmarketos/contents/images/2000x2000.png`.
   Не хватает также `rectangular_dropshadow.sci` (есть `rectangular_dropshadow@30.sci`) — мелочь, влияет на тени.
Другие замечания: `EGL display doesn't support EGL_WL_bind_wayland_display` (не критично), в `/etc/xdg/autostart/lomiri-indicator-network.desktop` ошибка разбора `Exec=`, при включении Lomiri стартуют `hfd-service` и `sensorfwd`.
Состояние на конец записи: служба `m52xq-lomiri.service` active, процессы `lomiri`, `superd`, `lomiri-telephony-service`; по словам пользователя на экране серый фон.
Возврат на Plasma: `m52xq-ui-switch plasma` (по ssh `172.16.42.1`) или кнопка выбора интерфейса.

"""
assert anchor in s
s = s.replace(anchor, add + anchor)
open(p, 'w').write(s)
EOF
echo ok
```

**22:19** — Ставлю обёртку locale для Lomiri и перезапускаю службу

```sh
B=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002/extra-files
W=$B/usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin; mkdir -p $W
cat > $W/locale <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# Только для службы m52xq-lomiri: плагин языков Lomiri берёт список из `locale -a`
# и ждёт имена с суффиксом .utf8; на musl их нет, поэтому список был пуст.
if [ "$1" = "-a" ]; then
    printf 'en_US.utf8\nru_RU.utf8\n'
else
    exec /usr/bin/locale "$@"
fi
EOF
chmod 755 $W/locale
sed -i 's|^Environment=LANG=C.UTF-8|Environment=LANG=C.UTF-8\nEnvironment=PATH=/usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin:/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin|' $B/usr/lib/systemd/system/m52xq-lomiri.service; sed -n 20,26p $B/usr/lib/systemd/system/m52xq-lomiri.service
cd $B && tar -cf - usr/local/lib/m52xq usr/lib/systemd/system/m52xq-lomiri.service | sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf - && chmod 755 /usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/locale && ls -l /usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin && systemctl daemon-reload && systemctl reset-failed m52xq-lomiri.service; systemctl restart m52xq-lomiri.service; sleep 35; systemctl is-active m52xq-lomiri.service; journalctl --since "-38s" --no-pager | grep -E "Wizard|Fatal|SEGV|ABRT|maliit" | sed "s/^.*\] //" | cut -c1-200 | head'
```

```sh
#!/bin/sh
P=$(pgrep -x lomiri | head -1)
E=$(tr "\0" "\n" < /proc/$P/environ | grep -E "^(DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS|XDG_RUNTIME_DIR|HOME|WAYLAND_DISPLAY|LANG|XDG_SEAT|XDG_SESSION_ID)=" | tr "\n" " ")
for n in power datetime session messages bluetooth; do
  if pgrep -f "[a]yatana-indicator-$n-service" >/dev/null; then echo "$n уже запущен"; continue; fi
  su user -s /bin/sh -c "env $E setsid nohup /usr/libexec/ayatana-indicator-$n/ayatana-indicator-$n-service >/tmp/ind-$n.log 2>&1 &"
done
sleep 5
pgrep -a -f "[a]yatana-indicator-.*-service" | cut -c1-90
EOF
sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'sh -s' < /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/ind.sh
```

**22:34** — Обновляю заглушку Greeter, перезапускаю Lomiri и индикаторы

```sh
B=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002/extra-files; G=$B/usr/lib/lomiri/qml/LightDM/IntegratedLightDM/Greeter.qml
cat > $G <<'EOF'
// Заглушка LightDM.IntegratedLightDM для postmarketOS (в Alpine-сборке lomiri нет нативного плагина).
// Пароля на телефоне нет: любой запрос входа сразу считается успешным, экран блокировки снимается сам.
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick 2.15

QtObject {
    id: greeter

    property bool active: false
    property bool authenticated: true
    property string authenticationUser: ""
    property string defaultSession: "lomiri"
    property string selectUser: ""
    property bool promptless: true
    property bool hasGuestAccount: false

    signal showGreeter()
    signal hideGreeter()
    signal loginError(bool automatic)
    signal loginSuccess(bool automatic)
    signal requestAuthenticationUser(string user)

    // сигнал отправляем не из самого вызова authenticate(), а чуть позже, чтобы не попасть в середину обработчика оболочки
    property Timer loginTimer: Timer {
        interval: 50
        onTriggered: greeter.loginSuccess(false)
    }

    function authenticate(user) {
        authenticationUser = user;
        authenticated = true;
        loginTimer.restart();
    }
    function respond(response) { loginTimer.restart(); }
    function startSessionSync(session) { return true; }
    function hideSessionChooser() {}
    function showGuestAccount() {}
}
EOF
cd $B && tar -cf - usr/lib/lomiri/qml/LightDM | sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf - && chmod 644 /usr/lib/lomiri/qml/LightDM/IntegratedLightDM/*; systemctl reset-failed m52xq-lomiri.service; systemctl restart m52xq-lomiri.service; sleep 40; systemctl is-active m52xq-lomiri.service; journalctl --since "-42s" --no-pager | grep -E "Greeter.qml|LightDM" | sed "s/^.*\] //" | cut -c1-200 | head -5'; sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'sh -s' < /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/ind.sh | wc -l
```

**22:36** — Провожу виртуальный свайп сверху вниз через uinput

```sh
cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/swipe.py <<'EOF'
import os, struct, fcntl, time, sys
EV_SYN,EV_KEY,EV_ABS=0,1,3
ABS_MT_SLOT,ABS_MT_TRACKING_ID,ABS_MT_X,ABS_MT_Y=0x2f,0x39,0x35,0x36
BTN_TOUCH=0x14a
# границы настоящего тачскрина
rd=os.open('/dev/input/event3',os.O_RDONLY)
def absinfo(code):
    b=bytearray(24); fcntl.ioctl(rd,0x80184540+code,b); return struct.unpack('iiiiii',bytes(b))
ax=absinfo(ABS_MT_X); ay=absinfo(ABS_MT_Y); os.close(rd)
mx,my=ax[2],ay[2]
print('max x,y =',mx,my,flush=True)
fd=os.open('/dev/uinput',os.O_WRONLY|os.O_NONBLOCK)
def io(req,val=0): fcntl.ioctl(fd,req,val)
UI_SET_EVBIT,UI_SET_KEYBIT,UI_SET_ABSBIT,UI_SET_PROPBIT=0x40045564,0x40045565,0x40045567,0x4004556e
io(UI_SET_EVBIT,EV_KEY);io(UI_SET_EVBIT,EV_ABS);io(UI_SET_EVBIT,EV_SYN)
io(UI_SET_KEYBIT,BTN_TOUCH);io(UI_SET_PROPBIT,1)  # INPUT_PROP_DIRECT
for a in (ABS_MT_SLOT,ABS_MT_TRACKING_ID,ABS_MT_X,ABS_MT_Y): io(UI_SET_ABSBIT,a)
name=b'm52xq-test-swipe'.ljust(80,b'\0')
absmax=[0]*64;absmin=[0]*64
absmax[ABS_MT_SLOT]=9;absmax[ABS_MT_TRACKING_ID]=65535;absmax[ABS_MT_X]=mx;absmax[ABS_MT_Y]=my
dev=name+struct.pack('HHHH',3,0x1234,0x5678,1)+struct.pack('i',0)+struct.pack('64i',*absmax)+struct.pack('64i',*absmin)+struct.pack('64i',*[0]*64)+struct.pack('64i',*[0]*64)
os.write(fd,dev); io(0x5501)  # UI_DEV_CREATE
time.sleep(2)
def ev(t,c,v): os.write(fd,struct.pack('llHHi',int(time.time()),0,t,c,v))
def syn(): ev(EV_SYN,0,0)
x=mx//2; y0=2; y1=int(my*0.6)
ev(EV_ABS,ABS_MT_SLOT,0);ev(EV_ABS,ABS_MT_TRACKING_ID,77);ev(EV_ABS,ABS_MT_X,x);ev(EV_ABS,ABS_MT_Y,y0);ev(EV_KEY,BTN_TOUCH,1);syn()
steps=30
for i in range(1,steps+1):
    time.sleep(0.016*2)
    ev(EV_ABS,ABS_MT_X,x);ev(EV_ABS,ABS_MT_Y,y0+(y1-y0)*i//steps);syn()
time.sleep(0.3)
ev(EV_ABS,ABS_MT_TRACKING_ID,-1);ev(EV_KEY,BTN_TOUCH,0);syn()
time.sleep(1)
io(0x5502)  # UI_DEV_DESTROY
print('свайп отправлен',flush=True)
EOF
sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'ls -l /dev/uinput; python3 -' < /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/swipe.py 2>&1 | tail -8
```

**22:37** — Ставлю обёртку lomiri (full-shell + индикаторы), перезапускаю службу

```sh
B=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002/extra-files; W=$B/usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin
cat > $W/lomiri <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# Только для службы m52xq-lomiri (каталог стоит первым в её PATH, start_lomiri вызывает "lomiri" по PATH).
# 1) режим full-shell: экран блокировки снимается сам (пароля на телефоне нет, настоящего LightDM тоже нет);
# 2) службы индикаторов верхней панели: в Alpine они запускаются автозапуском XDG, а здесь его нет.
(
    sleep 4
    for n in power datetime session messages bluetooth; do
        [ -x /usr/libexec/ayatana-indicator-$n/ayatana-indicator-$n-service ] &&
            /usr/libexec/ayatana-indicator-$n/ayatana-indicator-$n-service &
    done
    wait
) >/dev/null 2>&1 &
exec /usr/bin/lomiri --mode=full-shell "$@"
EOF
chmod 755 $W/lomiri
cd $B && tar -cf - usr/local/lib/m52xq | sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf - && chmod 755 /usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/*; for p in $(pgrep -f "[a]yatana-indicator-.*-service"); do kill $p; done; systemctl reset-failed m52xq-lomiri.service; systemctl restart m52xq-lomiri.service; sleep 45; systemctl is-active m52xq-lomiri.service; pgrep -a lomiri | head -2 | cut -c1-90; pgrep -a -f "[a]yatana-indicator-.*-service" | cut -c1-80; journalctl --since "-47s" --no-pager | grep -E "Mode argument|mode" | sed "s/^.*\] //" | head -3'
```

**22:40** — Обновляю обёртку lomiri (wayland для приложений, все индикаторы) и перезапускаю

```sh
B=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002/extra-files; W=$B/usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin
cat > $W/lomiri <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# Только для службы m52xq-lomiri (каталог стоит первым в её PATH, start_lomiri вызывает "lomiri" по PATH).
# 1) режим full-shell: экран блокировки снимается сам (пароля на телефоне нет, настоящего LightDM тоже нет);
# 2) QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland для приложений и служб, которые запускает Lomiri (иначе Qt берёт xcb и падает);
# 3) службы индикаторов верхней панели: в Alpine они запускаются автозапуском XDG, а здесь его нет.
dbus-update-activation-environment QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland GRID_UNIT_PX="$GRID_UNIT_PX"
(
    sleep 4
    for n in power datetime session messages bluetooth display sound notifications; do
        [ -x /usr/libexec/ayatana-indicator-$n/ayatana-indicator-$n-service ] &&
            /usr/libexec/ayatana-indicator-$n/ayatana-indicator-$n-service &
    done
    wait
) >/dev/null 2>&1 &
exec /usr/bin/lomiri --mode=full-shell "$@"
EOF
chmod 755 $W/lomiri
cd $B && tar -cf - usr/local/lib/m52xq | sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf - && chmod 755 /usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/*; systemctl reset-failed m52xq-lomiri.service; systemctl restart m52xq-lomiri.service; sleep 45; systemctl is-active m52xq-lomiri.service; pgrep -a -f "[a]yatana-indicator-.*-service" | sed "s|/usr/libexec/||" | cut -c1-70'
```

```sh
cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/pull.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/002 || exit 1
SSH="sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1"
echo "start $(date +%T)"
$SSH 'systemctl stop m52xq-lomiri.service; sleep 3; sync; sleep 2; sync; gzip -1 -c /run/m52xq-rootsrc/userdata/rootfs.img' | gunzip -c > rootfs.new.img.part
rc=$?
echo "copy rc=$rc $(date +%T)"
$SSH 'systemctl reset-failed m52xq-lomiri.service; systemctl start m52xq-lomiri.service; systemctl is-active m52xq-lomiri.service'
ls -l rootfs.new.img.part
EOF
chmod +x /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/pull.sh
```

**23:34** — Убираю пустой usr/sbin, усиливаю проверку и тестирую её на пустом каталоге

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/архив && rmdir .v41-zip-stage/mainline-extras/rootfs-files/usr/sbin && cat > в41/check-rootfs-files-paths.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# в41: run before building a zip: rootfs-files must NOT contain anything (files, links, even EMPTY DIRECTORIES) below or at a
# directory that is a symlink in the rootfs (usr/sbin -> bin, bin -> usr/bin, sbin -> usr/bin, lib -> usr/lib): TWRP's toybox
# `cp -a` fails with "Too many symbolic links encountered" and the installer prints "WARNING: cp of rootfs-files failed partway through"
# (v40: file usr/sbin/ModemManager; v41 first build: leftover empty directory usr/sbin). The top-level bin/lib/sbin are handled by
# update-binary; nested ones are not.
# Usage: check-rootfs-files-paths.sh <path to mainline-extras/rootfs-files>
d=${1:?rootfs-files directory}
bad=$(cd "$d" && find usr/sbin bin sbin 2>/dev/null)
if [ -n "$bad" ]; then echo "BAD (put them under the real directory, usr/sbin -> usr/bin; remove empty dirs):"; echo "$bad"; exit 1; fi
echo "rootfs-files paths ok"
EOF
chmod +x в41/check-rootfs-files-paths.sh && cp -p в41/check-rootfs-files-paths.sh /tmp/chk.sh && ./в41/check-rootfs-files-paths.sh .v41-zip-stage/mainline-extras/rootfs-files && echo "проверка на старом наборе (должна ругаться):" && mkdir -p /tmp/claude-1000/t/usr/sbin && ./в41/check-rootfs-files-paths.sh /tmp/claude-1000/t; rm -rf /tmp/claude-1000/t; find .v41-zip-stage -xtype l | head -3
```

**00:26** — Вырезаю центральные фрагменты и считаю условную резкость снимков

```sh
mkdir -p crops && python3 - <<'EOF'
from PIL import Image, ImageFilter
import numpy as np
names={'image_0001.jpg':'main16','image_0002.jpg':'main64a','image_0004.jpg':'main64b','image_0003.jpg':'front32','image_0005.jpg':'macro5','image_0006.jpg':'wide13'}
for f,n in names.items():
    im=Image.open(f); w,h=im.size
    c=im.crop((w//2-450,h//2-350,w//2+450,h//2+350)); c.save(f'crops/{n}.jpg',quality=90)
    g=np.asarray(im.convert('L').resize((w//4,h//4)),dtype=float)
    lap=np.abs(g[1:-1,1:-1]*4-g[:-2,1:-1]-g[2:,1:-1]-g[1:-1,:-2]-g[1:-1,2:]).mean()
    print(n,(w,h),'резкость(лапласиан,среднее): %.1f'%lap)
EOF
```

```sh
R=архив/.v41-zip-stage/mainline-extras/rootfs-files/usr/lib/modules/7.1.2/extra/venus; C=/home/sasha/pmbootstrap-work/chroot_native; for m in drivers/media/v4l2-core/v4l2-mem2mem.ko drivers/media/common/videobuf2/videobuf2-dma-contig.ko; do b=$(basename $m); [ -f linux/$m ] || { echo "нет в дереве: $m"; continue; }; sudo cp linux/$m $C/tmp/new-$b; sudo cp $R/$b $C/tmp/old-$b; sudo chroot $C /bin/sh -c "cd /tmp; llvm-objdump -d --no-show-raw-insn new-$b | tail -n +4 > n.dis; llvm-objdump -d --no-show-raw-insn old-$b | tail -n +4 > o.dis; echo \"$b: разных строк кода $(diff n.dis o.dis | grep -c '^[<>]')\""; done; ls -l --time-style=+%F linux/drivers/media/v4l2-core/v4l2-mem2mem.ko 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $6}'
```

**14:35** — Сравниваю машинный код модулей архива со свежей сборкой из текущего дерева

```sh
C=/home/sasha/pmbootstrap-work/chroot_native; sudo tee $C/tmp/cmpko.sh >/dev/null <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# usage: cmpko.sh new.ko old.ko -> number of differing disassembly lines (addresses/relocs ignored only via same layout)
llvm-objdump -d --no-show-raw-insn "$1" | tail -n +4 > /tmp/n.dis
llvm-objdump -d --no-show-raw-insn "$2" | tail -n +4 > /tmp/o.dis
echo "$(basename $2): $(diff /tmp/n.dis /tmp/o.dis | grep -c '^[<>]') разных строк"
EOF
sudo chmod +x $C/tmp/cmpko.sh; cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн; R=архив/.v41-zip-stage/mainline-extras/rootfs-files; for p in "drivers/media/v4l2-core/v4l2-mem2mem.ko:usr/lib/modules/7.1.2/extra/venus/v4l2-mem2mem.ko" "drivers/media/common/videobuf2/videobuf2-dma-contig.ko:usr/lib/modules/7.1.2/extra/venus/videobuf2-dma-contig.ko" "drivers/net/wireless/ath/ath11k/ath11k_ahb.ko:lib/modules/7.1.2/kernel/drivers/net/wireless/ath/ath11k/ath11k_ahb.ko"; do n=${p%%:*}; o=${p#*:}; b=$(basename $n); sudo cp linux/$n $C/tmp/new-$b; sudo cp $R/$o $C/tmp/old-$b; sudo chroot $C /tmp/cmpko.sh /tmp/new-$b /tmp/old-$b; done
```

**18:38** — Готовлю обёртку сеанса Lomiri, файл сеанса, fstab и ориентацию для 003, смотрю ход установки

```sh
X=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/extra-003; mkdir -p $X/usr/local/bin $X/usr/share/wayland-sessions $X/usr/share/glib-2.0/schemas $X/etc
cat > $X/usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# m52xq (убунтутач24/003): the Lomiri session tinydm starts (usr/share/wayland-sessions/m52xq-lomiri.desktop).
# On 002 these came from m52xq-lomiri.service; here tinydm runs the session, so they are set here:
#   PATH first: usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin (lomiri --mode=full-shell + the indicator services; locale -a with .utf8)
#   QML2_IMPORT_PATH: the Lomiri.SystemSettings.* modules (and our Update stub) for the first-run wizard
#   GRID_UNIT_PX=21: the UI scale of a 6.7" 1080x2400 phone (lomiri-deviceinfo alone is not read by the UI toolkit)
export LANG="${LANG:-C.UTF-8}"
export PATH="/usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin:${PATH:-/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin}"
export QML2_IMPORT_PATH="/usr/lib/lomiri-system-settings/private${QML2_IMPORT_PATH:+:$QML2_IMPORT_PATH}"
export GRID_UNIT_PX=21
exec /usr/bin/start_lomiri "$@"
EOF
chmod 755 $X/usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri
cat > $X/usr/share/wayland-sessions/m52xq-lomiri.desktop <<'EOF'
[Desktop Entry]
Name=Lomiri (m52xq)
Comment=Lomiri with the Samsung M52 settings (m52xq-start-lomiri)
Exec=/usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri
Type=Application
DesktopNames=Lomiri
EOF
cat > $X/usr/share/glib-2.0/schemas/99-m52xq-lomiri.gschema.override <<'EOF'
[com.lomiri.touch.system]
orientation-lock='PortraitOrientation'
EOF
cat > $X/etc/fstab <<'EOF'
# m52xq (убунтутач24/003): this rootfs lives as /data/rootfs.img; the root and /boot are mounted by the initramfs hook.
# Shared with the main (Plasma) system on userdata: logs, coredumps and the apk cache. The home is NOT shared
# (it stays in this image), so the Lomiri settings do not mix with the Plasma ones.
/dev/disk/by-partlabel/userdata /mnt/userdata ext4 defaults,nofail,x-systemd.device-timeout=5s 0 0
/mnt/userdata/varlog /var/log none bind,nofail,x-systemd.requires=mnt-userdata.mount,x-systemd.requires=m52xq-prep-userdata-varlog.service 0 0
/mnt/userdata/coredumps /var/lib/systemd/coredump none bind,nofail,x-systemd.requires=mnt-userdata.mount,x-systemd.requires=m52xq-prep-userdata-varlog.service 0 0
/mnt/userdata/apk-cache /var/cache/apk none bind,nofail,x-systemd.requires=mnt-userdata.mount,x-systemd.requires=m52xq-prep-userdata-varlog.service 0 0
EOF
sh -n $X/usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri && echo ok; find $X -type f; cat /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/tasks/by1v6jtho.output 2>/dev/null | tail -3; sudo tail -1 /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/install.log 2>/dev/null | cut -c1-100
```

**19:49** — Создаю фикс PATH для systemd, кладу его на телефон и проверяю md5

```sh
set -e
E=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/extra-003/etc/systemd
mkdir -p $E/system.conf.d $E/user.conf.d
cat > $E/system.conf.d/10-m52xq-path.conf <<'EOF'
# M52XQ 003: a fresh edge install has real /bin and /sbin (busybox applets live only there), but the default PATH of
# systemd has no /bin and /sbin: every shell script started by a service failed ("mkdir: not found" in tinydm-run-session)
[Manager]
DefaultEnvironment="PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"
EOF
cp $E/system.conf.d/10-m52xq-path.conf $E/user.conf.d/
md5sum $E/system.conf.d/10-m52xq-path.conf
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/extra-003
tar -cf - etc/systemd/system.conf.d etc/systemd/user.conf.d | sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf - && chown -R 0:0 /etc/systemd/system.conf.d /etc/systemd/user.conf.d && md5sum /etc/systemd/system.conf.d/10-m52xq-path.conf /etc/systemd/user.conf.d/10-m52xq-path.conf && sync'
```

**20:33** — Пишу генератор картинок заставки

```sh
mkdir -p /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/splash; cat > /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/splash/make-splash.py <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# убунтутач24/003: images of the Plymouth theme m52xq-ubuntu-touch (the Ubuntu Touch boot splash: pink background,
# white "ubuntu touch" logo, 5 dots). Usage: make-splash.py <theme dir>
import math, sys
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont, ImageFilter
out = sys.argv[1]
W, H = 1080, 2400
F = '/usr/share/fonts/truetype/ubuntu/'

# background: violet (left/top) -> pink (right/bottom), with soft lighter facets like the UT splash
bg = Image.new('RGB', (W, H))
a, b = (178, 28, 182), (236, 20, 140)
px = bg.load()
for y in range(H):
    for x in range(W):
        t = min(1.0, max(0.0, (x / W) * 0.55 + (y / H) * 0.45))
        px[x, y] = tuple(int(a[i] + (b[i] - a[i]) * t) for i in range(3))
fac = Image.new('RGBA', (W, H), (0, 0, 0, 0))
d = ImageDraw.Draw(fac)
d.polygon([(0, 0), (W * 0.62, 0), (0, H * 0.42)], fill=(255, 255, 255, 14))
d.polygon([(W, H * 0.30), (W, H), (W * 0.25, H)], fill=(255, 255, 255, 12))
d.polygon([(W * 0.35, H * 0.55), (W, H * 0.20), (W, H * 0.62)], fill=(80, 0, 90, 18))
bg = Image.alpha_composite(bg.convert('RGBA'), fac).convert('RGB')
bg.save(out + '/background.png', optimize=True)

# logo: "ubuntu" (Ubuntu Medium) + circle of friends, "touch" under the right end
fu = ImageFont.truetype(F + 'Ubuntu-M.ttf', 150)
ft = ImageFont.truetype(F + 'Ubuntu-R.ttf', 70)
lw, lh = 680, 260
logo = Image.new('RGBA', (lw * 2, lh * 2), (0, 0, 0, 0))   # drawn at 2x, scaled down for smooth edges
d = ImageDraw.Draw(logo)
fu2 = ImageFont.truetype(F + 'Ubuntu-M.ttf', 300)
ft2 = ImageFont.truetype(F + 'Ubuntu-R.ttf', 140)
d.text((10, 0), 'ubuntu', font=fu2, fill='white')
uw = d.textlength('ubuntu', font=fu2)
# circle of friends (registered-mark position)
cx, cy, r = 10 + uw + 50, 95, 40
d.ellipse((cx - r, cy - r, cx + r, cy + r), outline='white', width=12)
for k in range(3):
    ang = math.radians(-90 + 120 * k + 30)
    hx, hy = cx + r * math.cos(ang), cy + r * math.sin(ang)
    d.ellipse((hx - 15, hy - 15, hx + 15, hy + 15), fill='white')
tw = d.textlength('touch', font=ft2)
d.text((10 + uw - tw + 10, 270), 'touch', font=ft2, fill='white')
logo = logo.crop(logo.getbbox()).resize((lw, int(lw * logo.getbbox()[3] / logo.getbbox()[2])) if False else None or logo.crop(logo.getbbox()).size, Image.LANCZOS)
bb = logo.getbbox(); logo = logo.crop(bb)
logo = logo.resize((logo.width // 2, logo.height // 2), Image.LANCZOS)
logo.save(out + '/logo.png', optimize=True)

# dots: dim and bright
for name, col, rr in (('dot-off.png', (255, 190, 120, 150), 9), ('dot-on.png', (255, 255, 255, 255), 11)):
    im = Image.new('RGBA', (32, 32), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    ImageDraw.Draw(im).ellipse((16 - rr, 16 - rr, 16 + rr, 16 + rr), fill=col)
    im.save(out + '/' + name)
EOF
echo ok
```

**20:34** — Пишу файл темы и скрипт анимации Plymouth

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/splash; T=theme/m52xq-ubuntu-touch
cat > $T/m52xq-ubuntu-touch.plymouth <<'EOF'
[Plymouth Theme]
Name=Ubuntu Touch (M52XQ)
Description=Ubuntu Touch boot splash: pink background, white logo, 5 running dots
ModuleName=script

[script]
ImageDir=/usr/share/plymouth/themes/m52xq-ubuntu-touch
ScriptFile=/usr/share/plymouth/themes/m52xq-ubuntu-touch/m52xq-ubuntu-touch.script
EOF
cat > $T/m52xq-ubuntu-touch.script <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003: Ubuntu Touch boot splash (images: 003/build/splash/make-splash.py)

W = Window.GetWidth();
H = Window.GetHeight();

bg = Image("background.png").Scale(W, H);
bg_sprite = Sprite(bg);
bg_sprite.SetZ(-100);

logo = Image("logo.png");
logo_scale = (W * 0.62) / logo.GetWidth();
logo = logo.Scale(logo.GetWidth() * logo_scale, logo.GetHeight() * logo_scale);
logo_sprite = Sprite(logo);
logo_y = H / 2 - logo.GetHeight() / 2;
logo_sprite.SetPosition(W / 2 - logo.GetWidth() / 2, logo_y, 0);

dot_on = Image("dot-on.png");
dot_off = Image("dot-off.png");
step = W * 0.045;
dots_y = logo_y + logo.GetHeight() + H * 0.03;
for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    dot[i] = Sprite(dot_off);
    dot[i].SetPosition(W / 2 + (i - 2) * step - dot_off.GetWidth() / 2, dots_y, 1);
}

# the bright dot runs left to right, one position every 0.4 s (refresh is 50 Hz)
frame = 0;
fun refresh_callback() {
    on = Math.Int(frame / 20) % 5;
    for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        if (i == on)
            dot[i].SetImage(dot_on);
        else
            dot[i].SetImage(dot_off);
    }
    frame++;
}
Plymouth.SetRefreshFunction(refresh_callback);

# messages (fsck, shutdown) under the dots
msg_sprite = Sprite();
msg_sprite.SetPosition(W * 0.05, H * 0.85, 2);
fun message_callback(text) {
    msg_sprite.SetImage(Image.Text(text, 1, 1, 1));
}
Plymouth.SetMessageFunction(message_callback);
EOF
ls $T
```

**20:37** — Создаю пользовательские юниты, ставлю их на телефон и проверяю запуск настроек Bluetooth

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/build/extra-003; U=etc/systemd/user; mkdir -p $U
mk() { cat > $U/$1 <<EOF
# убунтутач24/003: Alpine ships the D-Bus activation file of $2 with SystemdService=$1 but no such systemd user unit:
# dbus-broker then fails every activation ("unknown unit"), e.g. indicator menu items (url-dispatcher) did nothing
[Unit]
Description=$2 (D-Bus activated)
PartOf=graphical-session.target

[Service]
Type=dbus
BusName=$2
ExecStart=$3
EOF
}
mk lomiri-url-dispatcher.service com.lomiri.URLDispatcher /usr/libexec/lomiri-url-dispatcher/lomiri-url-dispatcher
mk lomiri-download-manager-systemd.service com.lomiri.applications.Downloader /usr/bin/lomiri-download-manager
mk lomiri-upload-manager-systemd.service com.lomiri.applications.Uploader /usr/bin/lomiri-upload-manager
mk lomiri-telephony-service-handler.service com.lomiri.TelephonyServiceHandler /usr/bin/lomiri-telephony-service-handler
mk lomiri-telephony-service-approver.service org.freedesktop.Telepathy.Client.TelephonyServiceApprover /usr/bin/lomiri-telephony-service-approver
mk lomiri-indicator-telephony-service.service org.freedesktop.Telepathy.Client.TelephonyServiceIndicator /usr/bin/lomiri-telephony-service-indicator
ls $U; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - $U/lomiri-*.service | sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf - && su user -s /bin/sh -c "export DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=unix:path=/run/user/10000/bus XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/run/user/10000; systemctl --user daemon-reload; timeout 20 gdbus call --session --dest com.lomiri.URLDispatcher --object-path /com/lomiri/URLDispatcher --method com.lomiri.URLDispatcher.DispatchURL settings:///system/bluetooth \"\"; sleep 5; systemctl --user status lomiri-url-dispatcher --no-pager | tail -8" 2>&1 | cut -c1-200; pgrep -a lomiri-system-settings'
```

**20:53** — Читаю разметку GPT образа Plasma в35

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/архив/в35/data/rootfs-chunks; ls -laL; head -c 8388608 samsung-m52xq.img.part-0 > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/gpt-head.bin; sgdisk -p /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/gpt-head.bin 2>&1 | head -5; python3 - <<'EOF'
import struct
d=open('/tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/gpt-head.bin','rb').read()
h=d[4096:4096+92]; print(h[:8], 'entries lba',struct.unpack('<Q',h[72:80])[0],'n',struct.unpack('<I',h[80:84])[0],'sz',struct.unpack('<I',h[84:88])[0], 'first/last usable', struct.unpack('<QQ',h[40:56]))
el=struct.unpack('<Q',h[72:80])[0]*4096
for i in range(4):
    e=d[el+i*128:el+(i+1)*128]
    if e[:16]==b'\0'*16: continue
    s,l=struct.unpack('<QQ',e[32:48]); print(i+1, s, l, (l-s+1)*4096/2**20,'MiB', e[56:128].decode('utf-16le').rstrip('\0'))
EOF
```

**20:57** — Меняю версию в баннере, исправляю владельца реестра датчиков и добавляю файл-метку

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/archive; U=META-INF/com/google/android/update-binary; python3 - $U <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
a=s.count('ui_print " postmarketOS mainline plasma v42 — m52xq"')
s=s.replace('ui_print " postmarketOS mainline plasma v42 — m52xq"','ui_print " postmarketOS mainline lomiri v1 — m52xq"')
old='                chown -R 108:113 "$SNS_REG"\n'
new='''                # lomiri v1: the fastrpc ids differ between images (Plasma 108:113, this Lomiri-only image 103:105):
                # take them from the rootfs being flashed
                FRPC_UID=$(awk -F: '$1=="fastrpc"{print $3}' "$ROOTFS_MNT/etc/passwd")
                FRPC_GID=$(awk -F: '$1=="fastrpc"{print $3}' "$ROOTFS_MNT/etc/group")
                chown -R "${FRPC_UID:-108}:${FRPC_GID:-113}" "$SNS_REG"
'''
b=s.count(old); s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s); print(a,b)
EOF
grep -n "lomiri v1\|FRPC" $U; cat > mainline-extras/rootfs-files/etc/m52xq/lomiri-v1 <<'EOF'
postmarketOS mainline + Lomiri (no Plasma) for Samsung Galaxy M52 5G (m52xq), archive lomiri v1.
The rootfs image already holds every m52xq file; this zip injects nothing else. The installer only adds this phone's own
data here: Bluetooth address (EFS), modem firmware (modem partition), sensor registry (persist).
EOF
find . -type f | sort
```

**21:05** — Собираю образ GPT с разделами pmOS_boot из в35 и рутфс Lomiri и проверяю его

```sh
set -e; cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003; R=rootfs-from-phone.img; P2=$((1178177*4096)); truncate -s $P2 $R; P2S=$((P2/4096)); START2=124928; END2=$((START2+P2S-1)); TOTAL=$(( ( (END2+1+6)*4096 + 1048575 ) / 1048576 * 1048576 )); echo p2 sectors=$P2S end=$END2 total=$TOTAL bytes $((TOTAL/1048576)) MiB
O=samsung-m52xq-lomiri.img; rm -f $O; truncate -s $TOTAL $O
# p1 = pmOS_boot from the в35 Plasma image (the /boot that 003 boots with today)
dd if=../../../архив/в35/data/rootfs-chunks/samsung-m52xq.img.part-0 of=$O bs=4096 skip=2048 seek=2048 count=$((124927-2048+1)) conv=notrunc status=none
dd if=$R of=$O bs=4M seek=$((START2*4096/4194304)) conv=notrunc,sparse status=none 2>/dev/null || dd if=$R of=$O bs=4096 seek=$START2 conv=notrunc,sparse status=none
LOOP=$(sudo losetup --show -f --sector-size 4096 $O); echo $LOOP
sudo sgdisk -o -n 1:2048:124927 -t 1:C12A7328-F81F-11D2-BA4B-00A0C93EC93B -c 1:primary -n 2:$START2:$END2 -t 2:B921B045-1DF0-41C3-AF44-4C6F280D3FAE -c 2:primary $LOOP
sudo sgdisk -p $LOOP | tail -4; sudo partprobe $LOOP 2>/dev/null || true; sudo losetup -d $LOOP
LOOP=$(sudo losetup --show -Pf --sector-size 4096 $O); sleep 1; sudo blkid ${LOOP}p1 ${LOOP}p2; sudo e2fsck -fn ${LOOP}p2 2>&1 | tail -1; sudo e2fsck -fn ${LOOP}p1 2>&1 | tail -1; sudo losetup -d $LOOP; ls -la $O
```

**21:08** — Пишу описание архива и проверяю папку на симлинки

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/archive; cat > ЧТО_НОВОГО.txt <<'EOF'
postmarketOS mainline + Lomiri (без Plasma) для Samsung Galaxy M52 5G (m52xq) — архив lomiri v1 (2026-10-25)

Ставится в раздел system (как архивы Plasma вNN): boot v241 (ядро #430, NFC) + vendor_boot v238 + образ GPT
(p1 pmOS_boot из в35, p2 pmOS_root — рутфс Lomiri 4,5 ГиБ, при первой загрузке растягивается на весь system).
Рутфс снят с телефона (убунтутач24/003, postmarketOS edge, 1102 пакета), личные данные удалены: при установке
адрес Bluetooth, прошивка модема и реестр датчиков берутся с самого телефона (EFS, modem, persist).

Что работает в Lomiri: мастер первого запуска пройден (русский), портрет, клавиатура maliit, касания в приложениях,
меню шторки (url-dispatcher), лаунчер (Morph, Галерея, Megapixels, Звонки, SMS, Настройки, Файлы, Терминал, Часы,
Калькулятор), заставка Ubuntu Touch при загрузке, обои Focal Fossa, кнопка лаунчера с логотипом Ubuntu Touch.
Железо — как в Plasma v42 (Wi-Fi, BT, звук, модем, GPS, датчики, камера, NFC).

Не сделано: экран блокировки с кругом (Lomiri в режиме full-shell, блокировка снимается сама), автоповорот
(iio-sensor-proxy выключен: из-за него окна приложений поворачивались и не принимали касания), звонки из
интерфейса Lomiri (нужен telepathy-ofono, модем у нас через ModemManager).

ВАЖНО: если на /data лежит rootfs.img, загрузчик возьмёт его, а не system — перед прошивкой его убрать/переименовать.
Пароль user и root: <пароль>.
EOF
find . -xtype l; find . -type l; ls -R | head -30; du -sh .
```

**23:19** — Правлю копию OrientedShell.qml: кнопки питания и громкости не считаются клавиатурами

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003; D=в2/rootfs-files/usr/share/lomiri; mkdir -p $D; timeout 40 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat /usr/share/lomiri/OrientedShell.qml' > $D/OrientedShell.qml.orig; cp $D/OrientedShell.qml.orig $D/OrientedShell.qml; python3 - $D/OrientedShell.qml <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
old="""    Binding {
        target: QuickUtils
        property: "keyboardAttached"
        value: keyboardsModel.count > 0"""
new="""    // m52xq: the power and volume keys are input devices with keys, so Lomiri counted them as hardware keyboards
    // and never showed the on-screen keyboard: only other keyboards count
    readonly property var buttonDeviceNames: ["pmic_pwrkey", "pmic_resin", "gpio-keys"]
    readonly property int realKeyboards: {
        var n = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < keyboardsModel.count; i++) {
            if (buttonDeviceNames.indexOf(keyboardsModel.get(i).name) === -1)
                n++;
        }
        return n;
    }

    Binding {
        target: QuickUtils
        property: "keyboardAttached"
        value: root.realKeyboards > 0"""
assert s.count(old)==1; s=s.replace(old,new)
o2="        hasKeyboard: keyboardsModel.count > 0\n"
assert s.count(o2)==1; s=s.replace(o2,"        hasKeyboard: root.realKeyboards > 0\n")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
diff $D/OrientedShell.qml.orig $D/OrientedShell.qml; grep -n "^Item\|^    id: root" $D/OrientedShell.qml | head -3
```

**23:24** — Добавляю в копию OrientedShell.qml таймер активации окна оболочки

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files/usr/share/lomiri; grep -n "^import" OrientedShell.qml; python3 - OrientedShell.qml <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
old="    Binding {\n        target: QuickUtils\n        property: \"keyboardAttached\""
new="""    // m52xq: Qt never got the shell window as its active (focus) window, so it never told the input context which
    // field has the focus (no setFocusObject) and maliit dropped every "show" request: activate the window ourselves
    Timer {
        interval: 2000
        repeat: true
        running: true
        onTriggered: {
            var w = QtQuickWindow.Window.window;
            if (w && !w.active) {
                console.log("m52xq: shell window not active, requesting activation");
                w.requestActivate();
            }
        }
    }

""" + old
assert s.count(old)==1; s=s.replace(old,new,1); open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
diff OrientedShell.qml.orig OrientedShell.qml | head -30
```

```sh
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
assert "ExecStart=/usr/sbin/ModemManager" in s

open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
cat $D/m52xq-quick-suspend.conf | tail -6; timeout 150 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat > /etc/systemd/system/ModemManager.service.d/m52xq-quick-suspend.conf; systemctl daemon-reload; systemctl restart ModemManager; sleep 3; systemctl show ModemManager -p ExecStart | cut -c1-120; T=$(date +%s); sleep 75; echo "creating in 75s: $(journalctl -u ModemManager --no-pager --since @$T | grep -c "creating modem")"; mmcli -L' < $D/m52xq-quick-suspend.conf 2>&1
```

**23:49** — Ставлю drop-in с нашим ModemManager r8, перезапускаю его и проверяю модем

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003; D=в2/rootfs-files/etc/systemd/system/ModemManager.service.d; rm -f $D/m52xq-quick-suspend.conf; cat > $D/zz-m52xq-modemmanager-r8.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003: our ModemManager r8 (dual-SIM qrtr patches) is /usr/bin/ModemManager. On this image /usr/sbin is a real
# directory with the plain edge package (r0), and postmarketos-base-ui-modemmanager-systemd (quick-suspend-resume.conf)
# starts that one: it kept re-probing the modem every 27 s ("SIM identifier has changed"). "zz-" sorts after it.
[Service]
ExecStart=
ExecStart=/usr/bin/ModemManager --test-quick-suspend-resume
EOF
timeout 150 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'F=/etc/systemd/system/ModemManager.service.d/m52xq-quick-suspend.conf; grep -v -E "^# убунтутач24/003|^# real directory" $F | sed "s#ExecStart=/usr/bin/ModemManager#ExecStart=/usr/sbin/ModemManager#" > /tmp/q; cat /tmp/q > $F; cat > /etc/systemd/system/ModemManager.service.d/zz-m52xq-modemmanager-r8.conf; systemctl daemon-reload; systemctl restart ModemManager; sleep 3; systemctl show ModemManager -p ExecStart | cut -c1-100; T=$(date +%s); sleep 75; echo "creating in 75s: $(journalctl -u ModemManager --no-pager --since @$T | grep -c "creating modem")"; mmcli -L; md5sum /etc/systemd/system/ModemManager.service.d/*' < $D/zz-m52xq-modemmanager-r8.conf 2>&1; md5sum $D/zz-m52xq-modemmanager-r8.conf
```

```sh
EOF
```

**00:04** — Пишу скрипт m52xq-app-env и беру desktop-файл Morph с телефона

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003; mkdir -p в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/bin в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/share/applications; cat > в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# m52xq (убунтутач24/003): Lomiri passes its apps only part of its environment (QT_*, XDG_*, MIR_* ...), not GRID_UNIT_PX,
# so every Ubuntu Touch (UITK) app came up with the default 8 px grid unit, ~2.5 times too small. The app .desktop
# copies in /usr/local/share/applications start the app through this script.
export GRID_UNIT_PX="${GRID_UNIT_PX:-21}"
exec "$@"
EOF
chmod 755 в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env; timeout 40 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat /usr/share/applications/morph-browser.desktop' > в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/share/applications/morph-browser.desktop; grep -n -E "^(Exec|TryExec)=" в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/share/applications/morph-browser.desktop
```

**00:10** — Добавляю чтение языка из AccountsService в скрипт запуска Lomiri и m52xq-app-env

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; timeout 30 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat /usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri' > usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri; python3 - usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
old='export LANG="${LANG:-C.UTF-8}"\n'
new='''#   LANG: the language chosen in the first-run wizard / System Settings (AccountsService "Languages"); without it the
#   session ran with the en_US.UTF-8 of the login environment and System Settings and the shade stayed English
m52xq_lang=$(sed -n 's/^Languages=\\([^;]*\\).*/\\1/p' "/var/lib/AccountsService/users/$(id -un)" 2>/dev/null)
if [ -n "$m52xq_lang" ]; then
	export LANG="${m52xq_lang%%.*}.UTF-8" LANGUAGE="${m52xq_lang%%.*}"
else
	export LANG="${LANG:-C.UTF-8}"
fi
'''
assert s.count(old)==1; s=s.replace(old,new); open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
python3 - usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('export GRID_UNIT_PX="${GRID_UNIT_PX:-21}"\n','''export GRID_UNIT_PX="${GRID_UNIT_PX:-21}"
# the language is not passed either (System Settings and the apps stayed English): the one of the wizard / Settings
l=$(sed -n 's/^Languages=\\([^;]*\\).*/\\1/p' "/var/lib/AccountsService/users/$(id -un)" 2>/dev/null)
[ -n "$l" ] && export LANG="${l%%.*}.UTF-8" LANGUAGE="${l%%.*}"
''')
s=s.replace("# m52xq (убунтутач24/003): Lomiri passes its apps only part of its environment (QT_*, XDG_*, MIR_* ...), not GRID_UNIT_PX,","# m52xq (убунтутач24/003): Lomiri passes its apps only part of its environment (QT_*, XDG_*, MIR_* ...), not GRID_UNIT_PX or LANG,")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
cat usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env; sh -n usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env && sh -n usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri && echo syntax-ok; printf 'Languages=ru_RU;\n' > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/as; sed -n 's/^Languages=\([^;]*\).*/\1/p' /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/as
```

**00:10** — Ставлю исправленные скрипты, устанавливаю lomiri-sounds и перезапускаю Lomiri

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; python3 - usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri <<'EOF'
import sys
new_cmd='''$(busctl --system get-property org.freedesktop.Accounts "/org/freedesktop/Accounts/User$(id -u)" \\
	org.freedesktop.Accounts.User Language 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/^s "\\(.*\\)"$/\\1/p')'''
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    s=open(p).read()
    old1='''$(sed -n 's/^Languages=\\([^;]*\\).*/\\1/p' "/var/lib/AccountsService/users/$(id -un)" 2>/dev/null)'''
    assert s.count(old1)==1, p
    s=s.replace(old1,new_cmd)
    s=s.replace('(AccountsService "Languages")','(AccountsService "Language", read over D-Bus: the file is root-only)')
    open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
sh -n usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env && sh -n usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri && echo ok; grep -n -A2 "busctl" usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri | head; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri | timeout 200 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; chmod 755 /usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env /usr/local/bin/m52xq-start-lomiri; su user -s /bin/sh -c "/usr/local/bin/m52xq-app-env sh -c \"echo LANG=\\\$LANG LANGUAGE=\\\$LANGUAGE GRID=\\\$GRID_UNIT_PX\""; apk add lomiri-sounds 2>&1 | tail -1; ls /usr/share/sounds/; sync; systemctl stop tinydm; for i in $(seq 1 20); do pgrep -x lomiri >/dev/null || break; sleep 1; done; sleep 2; systemctl start tinydm; sleep 15; systemctl is-active tinydm; P=$(pgrep -x lomiri | head -1); tr "\0" "\n" < /proc/$P/environ | grep -E "^(LANG|LANGUAGE)="'
```

**00:21** — Кладу x-www-browser, передающий адреса url-dispatcher Lomiri, и закрываю Firefox

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; cat > usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/x-www-browser <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# m52xq (убунтутач24/003): the shade indicators (libayatana-common of Alpine is built without url-dispatcher) open
# "settings:///system/..." with the first browser of "x-www-browser,google-chrome,firefox,chromium", which was Firefox.
# This one is first in the PATH of the Lomiri session: every URL goes to the Lomiri URL dispatcher (settings: -> System
# Settings, http(s): -> Morph, started by Lomiri like from the launcher), gio open if the dispatcher does not take it.
for u in "$@"; do
	gdbus call --session --dest com.lomiri.URLDispatcher --object-path /com/lomiri/URLDispatcher \
		--method com.lomiri.URLDispatcher.DispatchURL "$u" "" >/dev/null 2>&1 || gio open "$u"
done
EOF
chmod 755 usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/x-www-browser; sh -n usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/x-www-browser && echo ok; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/x-www-browser | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; for p in $(pgrep -x firefox); do kill $p; done; P=$(pgrep -f ayatana-indicator-power-service | head -1); tr "\0" "\n" < /proc/$P/environ | grep ^PATH; md5sum /usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/x-www-browser; sync'; md5sum usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/x-www-browser
```

**00:23** — Создаю службу повторного запуска va_macro, ставлю и включаю её на телефоне

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; mkdir -p usr/local/sbin etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants; cat > usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# M52XQ: the sound card after a lost ADSP start race. va_macro (3370000.codec) is probed right after the APR service of
# the ADSP, sometimes before the audio PD is up: "q6afe cmd 0x100f4 returned error 0x16", "AFE failed to vote (3)",
# probe -110, and the driver never tries again, so rx/tx macro, SoundWire and the card wait for its fsgen clock forever
# ("no soundcards"). Retry the probe until the card is there (two boots in a row on убунтутач24/003, 25.10).
DEV=3370000.codec
for i in $(seq 40); do
	grep -q '^ *[0-9]' /proc/asound/cards 2>/dev/null && exit 0
	if [ ! -e /sys/bus/platform/devices/$DEV/driver ]; then
		echo $DEV > /sys/bus/platform/drivers_probe 2>/dev/null
		[ -e /sys/bus/platform/devices/$DEV/driver ] && echo "m52xq-audio-reprobe: $DEV bound (try $i)"
	fi
	sleep 3
done
echo "m52xq-audio-reprobe: still no sound card" >&2
exit 1
EOF
chmod 755 usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe; cat > etc/systemd/system/m52xq-audio-reprobe.service <<'EOF'
[Unit]
Description=M52XQ: retry the LPASS VA macro probe lost to the ADSP start race (sound card)
After=m52xq-adsp-start.service
Wants=m52xq-adsp-start.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe
TimeoutStartSec=150

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
EOF
ln -sfn ../m52xq-audio-reprobe.service etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants/m52xq-audio-reprobe.service; sh -n usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe && echo ok; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe etc/systemd/system/m52xq-audio-reprobe.service etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants/m52xq-audio-reprobe.service | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; systemctl daemon-reload; systemctl is-enabled m52xq-audio-reprobe; systemctl start m52xq-audio-reprobe; systemctl is-active m52xq-audio-reprobe; journalctl -u m52xq-audio-reprobe -b --no-pager | tail -3 | cut -c1-150; sync'
```

**00:25** — Заменяю обращения к типам запросов на числа в копиях PromptList.qml и Greeter.qml

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; G=usr/share/lomiri/Greeter; python3 - $G/PromptList.qml.orig $G/PromptList.qml $G/Greeter.qml.orig $G/Greeter.qml <<'EOF'
import sys
m={"Message":0,"Question":1,"Secret":2,"Button":3,"Error":4}
note="// m52xq: prompt types as numbers (our LightDM stub declares them as an enum, which a QML instance property does not expose:\n// LightDMService.prompts.Button etc. were undefined). 0 Message, 1 Question, 2 Secret, 3 Button, 4 Error\n"
for src,dst in ((sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2]),(sys.argv[3],sys.argv[4])):
    s=open(src).read()
    for k,v in m.items():
        s=s.replace("LightDMService.prompts.%s"%k, "%d /* %s */"%(v,k))
    i=s.index("import ")
    s=s[:i]+note+s[i:]
    open(dst,'w').write(s)
EOF
```

**00:26** — Пишу новую заглушку Greeter.qml с запросом-кнопкой

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; D=usr/lib/lomiri/qml/LightDM/IntegratedLightDM; cat > $D/Greeter.qml <<'EOF'
// Заглушка LightDM.IntegratedLightDM для postmarketOS (в Alpine-сборке lomiri нет нативного плагина).
// Пароля на телефоне нет. v2 (убунтутач24/003): экран блокировки с часами и кругом. authenticate() кладёт один запрос
// типа «кнопка» (Button = 3, вход без пароля) и не шлёт loginSuccess сам: Lomiri показывает обложку, свайп вызывает
// respond → Greeter.qml d.login() → startSessionSync(), и экран снимается. Раньше loginSuccess уходил сразу (режим
// full-shell), блокировки не было; без запросов вовсе Greeter ждал (waiting: prompts.count == 0) — белая шторка на 002.
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick 2.15
import LightDM.IntegratedLightDM 0.1 as LightDM

QtObject {
    id: greeter

    property bool active: false
    property bool authenticated: true
    property string authenticationUser: ""
    property string defaultSession: "lomiri"
    property string selectUser: ""
    property bool promptless: true
    property bool hasGuestAccount: false

    signal showGreeter()
    signal hideGreeter()
    signal loginError(bool automatic)
    signal loginSuccess(bool automatic)
    signal requestAuthenticationUser(string user)

    function authenticate(user) {
        authenticationUser = user;
        authenticated = true;
        LightDM.Prompts.clear();
        LightDM.Prompts.append({ "text": "", "type": 3 });	// Button: unlock without a password
    }
    function respond(response) { greeter.loginSuccess(false); }
    function startSessionSync(session) { return true; }
    function hideSessionChooser() {}
    function showGuestAccount() {}
}
EOF
diff $D/Greeter.qml.orig $D/Greeter.qml | head -40; timeout 30 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat /usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/lomiri | tail -3'
```

**00:37** — Пишу службу m52xq-powerkey для короткого нажатия кнопки питания

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; cat > usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/python3
# M52XQ (убунтутач24/003, Lomiri): short press of the power key. On Ubuntu Touch repowerd blanks the screen and locks it,
# but it gets the key from unity-system-compositor, which this image does not have, so a short press did nothing
# (Lomiri itself only handles the long press: its power dialog). Screen on -> Lomiri lock screen
# (org.gnome.ScreenSaver.Lock) + backlight 0 + touchscreen inhibited (no touches in the pocket); screen off -> backlight
# back + touchscreen on (the lock screen is there, unlock by swipe). Presses held longer than SHORT are left to Lomiri.
import glob, os, struct, subprocess, time

SHORT = 1.0	# s
BL = glob.glob('/sys/class/backlight/*/brightness')[0]
SAVED = '/run/m52xq-powerkey.brightness'
USER = 'user'


def find(name):
	for d in glob.glob('/sys/class/input/input*'):
		try:
			if open(d + '/name').read().strip() == name:
				return d
		except OSError:
			pass
	return None


def write(path, value):
	try:
		with open(path, 'w') as f:
			f.write(str(value))
	except OSError as e:
		print('m52xq-powerkey: %s: %s' % (path, e), flush=True)


def lock():
	uid = subprocess.run(['id', '-u', USER], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
	env = 'DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=unix:path=/run/user/%s/bus' % uid
	subprocess.run(['su', USER, '-s', '/bin/sh', '-c', env + ' gdbus call --session -d org.gnome.ScreenSaver'
			' -o /org/gnome/ScreenSaver -m org.gnome.ScreenSaver.Lock'],
		stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=10)


def screen_off(touch):
	b = open(BL).read().strip()
	if b != '0':
		write(SAVED, b)
	lock()
	time.sleep(0.3)	# let the lock screen draw before the backlight goes
	write(BL, 0)
	if touch:
		write(touch + '/inhibited', 1)
	print('m52xq-powerkey: screen off (brightness %s)' % b, flush=True)


def screen_on(touch):
	if touch:
		write(touch + '/inhibited', 0)
	try:
		b = open(SAVED).read().strip() or '180'
	except OSError:
		b = '180'
# … ещё 47 строк
```

**00:45** — Переписываю m52xq-powerkey с сигналом питания экрана для qtmir и ставлю на телефон

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; cp usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/m52xq-powerkey.v1; cat > usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/python3
# M52XQ (убунтутач24/003, Lomiri): what repowerd does on Ubuntu Touch, as far as this image needs it.
# 1) Short press of the power key: repowerd gets the key from unity-system-compositor, which this image does not have,
#    so a short press did nothing (Lomiri itself only handles the long press: its power dialog). Screen on -> Lomiri lock
#    screen (org.gnome.ScreenSaver.Lock) + backlight 0 + touchscreen inhibited (no touches in the pocket); screen off ->
#    backlight back + touchscreen on (the lock screen is there, unlock by swipe). Longer presses are left to Lomiri.
# 2) com.canonical.Unity.Screen DisplayPowerStateChange(status, reason) on the system bus: qtmir runs its orientation
#    sensor only after "display on" from it, so the screen never rotated (one reading at start, then nothing). Sent at
#    start, every 15 s while the screen is on (Lomiri restarts) and on every screen on / off.
import glob, os, struct, subprocess, threading, time
from gi.repository import Gio, GLib

SHORT = 1.0	# s
BL = glob.glob('/sys/class/backlight/*/brightness')[0]
SAVED = '/run/m52xq-powerkey.brightness'
USER = 'user'
NAME, PATH, IFACE = 'com.canonical.Unity.Screen', '/com/canonical/Unity/Screen', 'com.canonical.Unity.Screen'
bus = None


def log(msg):
	print('m52xq-powerkey: ' + msg, flush=True)


def find(name):
	for d in glob.glob('/sys/class/input/input*'):
		try:
			if open(d + '/name').read().strip() == name:
				return d
		except OSError:
			pass
	return None


def write(path, value):
	try:
		with open(path, 'w') as f:
			f.write(str(value))
	except OSError as e:
		log('%s: %s' % (path, e))


def screen_is_on():
	return open(BL).read().strip() != '0'


def power_state(on):
	def emit():
		bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange', GLib.Variant('(ii)', (1 if on else 0, 0)))
		return False
	GLib.idle_add(emit)


def lock():
	uid = subprocess.run(['id', '-u', USER], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
	env = 'DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=unix:path=/run/user/%s/bus' % uid
	subprocess.run(['su', USER, '-s', '/bin/sh', '-c', env + ' gdbus call --session -d org.gnome.ScreenSaver'
			' -o /org/gnome/ScreenSaver -m org.gnome.ScreenSaver.Lock'],
		stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=10)
# … ещё 72 строк
```

**00:47** — Добавляю в m52xq-powerkey слежение за датчиком и перезапуск датчика qtmir

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; python3 - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""#    start, every 15 s while the screen is on (Lomiri restarts) and on every screen on / off.
""","""#    start, every 15 s while the screen is on (Lomiri restarts) and on every screen on / off.
# 3) Rotation: qtmir creates its QOrientationSensor in the Mir thread, which gets no D-Bus signals: start() reads the
#    orientation once (synchronously), the later PropertiesChanged of iio-sensor-proxy never arrive. We watch
#    iio-sensor-proxy ourselves and on every new AccelerometerOrientation send "display off, display on": qtmir stops and
#    starts its sensor and reads the new orientation.
""")
s=s.replace("""def heartbeat():""","""def reorient():
	def emit():
		bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange', GLib.Variant('(ii)', (0, 0)))
		GLib.timeout_add(50, lambda: bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange',
			GLib.Variant('(ii)', (1, 0))) and False)
		return False
	GLib.idle_add(emit)


def watch_orientation():
	proxy = 'net.hadess.SensorProxy'
	last = [None]

	def changed(conn, sender, path, iface, signal, params):
		props = params.unpack()[1]
		o = props.get('AccelerometerOrientation')
		if o and o != last[0]:
			last[0] = o
			if screen_is_on():
				reorient()
				log('orientation ' + o)

	def claim(*_):
		try:
			bus.call_sync(proxy, '/net/hadess/SensorProxy', proxy, 'ClaimAccelerometer', None, None,
				Gio.DBusCallFlags.NONE, 5000, None)
			log('accelerometer claimed')
		except GLib.Error as e:
			log('ClaimAccelerometer: ' + e.message)
	bus.signal_subscribe(proxy, 'org.freedesktop.DBus.Properties', 'PropertiesChanged', '/net/hadess/SensorProxy',
		None, Gio.DBusSignalFlags.NONE, changed)
	# claim again whenever iio-sensor-proxy (re)starts
	Gio.bus_watch_name_on_connection(bus, proxy, Gio.BusNameWatcherFlags.NONE, claim, None)


def heartbeat():""")
s=s.replace("""	GLib.timeout_add_seconds(15, heartbeat)
""","""	GLib.timeout_add_seconds(15, heartbeat)
	watch_orientation()
""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
python3 -m py_compile usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey && echo ok; rm -rf usr/local/sbin/__pycache__; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; chmod 755 /usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey; systemctl restart m52xq-powerkey; sleep 4; systemctl is-active m52xq-powerkey; journalctl -u m52xq-powerkey -b --no-pager | tail -4 | cut -c1-140; sync'
```

**00:49** — Ставлю причины сигналов: поворот как Proximity, кнопка как PowerKey, и обновляю службу

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; python3 - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""def power_state(on):
	def emit():
		bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange', GLib.Variant('(ii)', (1 if on else 0, 0)))""","""# reasons of the Lomiri Powerd plugin: 0 Unknown, 1 Inactivity, 2 PowerKey, 3 Proximity (on "off" Lomiri shows the lock
# screen unless the reason is Proximity)
POWERKEY, PROXIMITY = 2, 3


def power_state(on, reason=POWERKEY):
	def emit():
		bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange', GLib.Variant('(ii)', (1 if on else 0, reason)))""")
s=s.replace("""		bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange', GLib.Variant('(ii)', (0, 0)))
		GLib.timeout_add(50, lambda: bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange',
			GLib.Variant('(ii)', (1, 0))) and False)""","""		# reason Proximity: Lomiri must not lock the screen for this off/on (it did on every rotation)
		bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange', GLib.Variant('(ii)', (0, PROXIMITY)))
		GLib.timeout_add(50, lambda: bus.emit_signal(None, PATH, IFACE, 'DisplayPowerStateChange',
			GLib.Variant('(ii)', (1, PROXIMITY))) and False)""")
s=s.replace("""def heartbeat():
	if screen_is_on():
		power_state(True)""","""def heartbeat():
	if screen_is_on():
		power_state(True, PROXIMITY)""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
grep -n -E "PROXIMITY|POWERKEY|power_state\(" usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey; python3 -m py_compile usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey && echo ok; rm -rf usr/local/sbin/__pycache__; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; chmod 755 /usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey; systemctl restart m52xq-powerkey; sleep 3; systemctl is-active m52xq-powerkey; md5sum /usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey; sync'; md5sum usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey
```

**00:52** — Пишу модель круга с фото по дням, ставлю на телефон и перезапускаю Lomiri

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; D=usr/lib/lomiri/qml/LightDM/IntegratedLightDM; timeout 30 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 "cat /$D/Infographic.qml" > $D/Infographic.qml.orig; cat > $D/Infographic.qml <<'EOF'
// Заглушка LightDM.IntegratedLightDM: данные круга на экране блокировки (в Ubuntu Touch их даёт lomiri-libusermetrics).
// v2 (убунтутач24/003): фото по дням — файлы в ~/Pictures (Megapixels, снимки экрана в Pictures/Screenshots):
// внутреннее кольцо — текущий месяц (до сегодня), внешнее — прошлый, точка — сегодня, по центру «Сегодня сделано N фото».
// nextDataSource()/readyForDataChange() переключают фото и снимки экрана.
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick 2.15
import Qt.labs.folderlistmodel 2.15

QtObject {
    id: info

    property string username: "user"
    property string label: ""
    property var firstMonth: []
    property var secondMonth: []
    property int currentDay: new Date().getDate() - 1

    property int source: 0	// 0 photos, 1 screenshots
    property int nextSource: 0
    readonly property bool russian: Qt.locale().name.indexOf("ru") === 0

    property FolderListModel photos: FolderListModel {
        folder: "file:///home/user/Pictures"
        nameFilters: ["*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.dng", "*.JPG"]
        showDirs: false
        onStatusChanged: if (status === FolderListModel.Ready) info.rebuild()
        onCountChanged: info.rebuild()
    }
    property FolderListModel screenshots: FolderListModel {
        folder: "file:///home/user/Pictures/Screenshots"
        nameFilters: ["*.png", "*.jpg"]
        showDirs: false
        onStatusChanged: if (status === FolderListModel.Ready) info.rebuild()
        onCountChanged: info.rebuild()
    }

    function scale(n) { return n > 0 ? Math.min(1.0, 0.3 + 0.12 * n) : 0.12; }

    function rebuild() {
        var m = source === 0 ? photos : screenshots;
        var now = new Date();
        var y = now.getFullYear(), mo = now.getMonth(), today = now.getDate();
        var daysNow = new Date(y, mo + 1, 0).getDate();
        var daysPrev = new Date(y, mo, 0).getDate();
        var cur = [], prev = [], i;
        for (i = 0; i < daysNow; i++) cur.push(0);
        for (i = 0; i < daysPrev; i++) prev.push(0);
        for (i = 0; i < m.count; i++) {
            var t = m.get(i, "fileModified");
            if (!t) continue;
            if (t.getFullYear() === y && t.getMonth() === mo)
                cur[t.getDate() - 1]++;
            else if ((t.getFullYear() === y && t.getMonth() === mo - 1) || (mo === 0 && t.getFullYear() === y - 1 && t.getMonth() === 11))
                prev[t.getDate() - 1]++;
        }
        var n = cur[today - 1];
        var f = [], s = [];
        for (i = 0; i < daysNow; i++) f.push(i < today ? scale(cur[i]) : undefined);
        for (i = 0; i < daysPrev; i++) s.push(scale(prev[i]));
# … ещё 23 строк
```

**00:58** — Делаю квадратные обои с котом и превью портрета и пейзажа

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; python3 - <<'EOF'
from PIL import Image, ImageFilter
im=Image.open('/usr/share/backgrounds/warty-final-ubuntu.png').convert('RGB')
W,H=im.size   # 4096x2304
S=4800
out=Image.new('RGB',(S,S))
px=(S-W)//2; py=(S-H)//2
def stretch(box,size): return im.crop(box).resize(size,Image.BILINEAR).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(50))
top=stretch((0,0,W,48),(W,py)); bot=stretch((0,H-48,W,H),(W,S-H-py))
out.paste(top,(px,0)); out.paste(bot,(px,py+H)); out.paste(im,(px,py))
left=out.crop((px,0,px+48,S)).resize((px,S),Image.BILINEAR).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(50))
right=out.crop((px+W-48,0,px+W,S)).resize((S-W-px,S),Image.BILINEAR).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(50))
out.paste(left,(0,0)); out.paste(right,(px+W,0))
for b in [(px-40,0,px+40,S),(px+W-40,0,px+W+40,S),(0,py-40,S,py+40),(0,py+H-40,S,py+H+40)]:
    out.paste(out.crop(b).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(24)),b)
out.save('/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files/usr/share/backgrounds/warty-final-ubuntu-square.png',optimize=True)
# previews of what Lomiri shows (PreserveAspectCrop)
lh=round(S*1080/2400); land=out.crop((0,(S-lh)//2,S,(S-lh)//2+lh)).resize((800,360))
pw=round(S*1080/2400); por=out.crop(((S-pw)//2,0,(S-pw)//2+pw,S)).resize((270,600))
c=Image.new('RGB',(800+20+270,600),'white'); c.paste(land,(0,0)); c.paste(por,(820,0))
c.save('/tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/catsq.png')
EOF
ls -la usr/share/backgrounds/
```

**00:58** — Переделываю края квадратных обоев зеркальным продолжением и делаю превью

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; python3 - <<'EOF'
from PIL import Image, ImageFilter, ImageOps
im=Image.open('/usr/share/backgrounds/warty-final-ubuntu.png').convert('RGB')
W,H=im.size
S=4800
px=(S-W)//2; py=(S-H)//2
# vertical: mirror the top/bottom bands of the picture, blurred (keeps the gradient going)
col=Image.new('RGB',(W,S))
col.paste(ImageOps.flip(im.crop((0,0,W,py))).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(60)),(0,0))
col.paste(ImageOps.flip(im.crop((0,H-(S-H-py),W,H))).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(60)),(0,py+H))
col.paste(im,(0,py))
for b in [(0,py-60,W,py+60),(0,py+H-60,W,py+H+60)]:
    col.paste(col.crop(b).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(30)),b)
out=Image.new('RGB',(S,S))
out.paste(ImageOps.mirror(col.crop((0,0,px,S))).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(60)),(0,0))
out.paste(ImageOps.mirror(col.crop((W-(S-W-px),0,W,S))).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(60)),(px+W,0))
out.paste(col,(px,0))
for b in [(px-60,0,px+60,S),(px+W-60,0,px+W+60,S)]:
    out.paste(out.crop(b).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(30)),b)
out.save('/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files/usr/share/backgrounds/warty-final-ubuntu-square.png',optimize=True)
lh=round(S*1080/2400); land=out.crop((0,(S-lh)//2,S,(S-lh)//2+lh)).resize((800,360))
pw=lh; por=out.crop(((S-pw)//2,0,(S-pw)//2+pw,S)).resize((270,600))
c=Image.new('RGB',(800+20+270,600),'white'); c.paste(land,(0,0)); c.paste(por,(820,0))
c.save('/tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/catsq2.png')
EOF
echo done
```

**00:59** — Делаю края квадратных обоев плавным градиентом и превью

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; python3 - <<'EOF'
from PIL import Image, ImageFilter
import numpy as np
im=Image.open('/usr/share/backgrounds/warty-final-ubuntu.png').convert('RGB')
a=np.asarray(im).astype(np.float32)
H,W,_=a.shape
S=4800
px=(S-W)//2; py=(S-H)//2; pb=S-H-py
mid=a[H//2-200:H//2+200].reshape(-1,3).mean(0)      # the purple of the picture
top=np.asarray(im.crop((0,0,W,8)).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(4))).astype(np.float32).mean(0)
bot=np.asarray(im.crop((0,H-8,W,H)).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(4))).astype(np.float32).mean(0)
t=np.linspace(1,0,py)[:,None,None]            # 1 at the very top -> 0 at the picture
T=top[None]*(1-t)+mid*0.85*t
t=np.linspace(0,1,pb)[:,None,None]
B=bot[None]*(1-t)+(bot.mean(0)*0.7)[None,None]*t
col=np.concatenate([T,a,B],0)
# horizontal: same idea per row
lc=col[:,:8].mean(1); rc=col[:,-8:].mean(1)
tl=np.linspace(1,0,px)[None,:,None]; tr=np.linspace(0,1,S-W-px)[None,:,None]
rowmean=col.mean(1)[:,None]
L=lc[:,None]*(1-tl)+rowmean*tl; R=rc[:,None]*(1-tr)+rowmean*tr
out=np.concatenate([L,col,R],1).clip(0,255).astype(np.uint8)
o=Image.fromarray(out)
for b in [(px-40,0,px+40,S),(px+W-40,0,px+W+40,S),(0,py-40,S,py+40),(0,py+H-40,S,py+H+40)]:
    o.paste(o.crop(b).filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(20)),b)
o.save('/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files/usr/share/backgrounds/warty-final-ubuntu-square.png',optimize=True)
lh=round(S*1080/2400); land=o.crop((0,(S-lh)//2,S,(S-lh)//2+lh)).resize((800,360))
por=o.crop(((S-lh)//2,0,(S-lh)//2+lh,S)).resize((270,600))
c=Image.new('RGB',(1090,600),'white'); c.paste(land,(0,0)); c.paste(por,(820,0))
c.save('/tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/catsq3.png')
EOF
echo done
```
