**01:02** — Добавляю пустые записи прошлого месяца для анимации круга и перезапускаю Lomiri

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; D=usr/lib/lomiri/qml/LightDM/IntegratedLightDM; python3 - $D/Infographic.qml <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
old="""        // only today: one circle (the rest of the month and the previous month stay without circles)
        for (i = 0; i < daysNow; i++) f.push(i === today - 1 ? (n > 0 ? Math.min(1.0, 0.6 + 0.08 * n) : 0.6) : undefined);"""
new="""        // only today: one circle (the rest of the month and the previous month stay without circles). The previous
        // month still needs its entries (undefined = no circle): Infographics.qml animates the present circles only after
        // it has gone through half of the past ones, so with an empty array today's circle never appeared
        for (i = 0; i < daysNow; i++) f.push(i === today - 1 ? (n > 0 ? Math.min(1.0, 0.6 + 0.08 * n) : 0.6) : undefined);
        for (i = 0; i < daysPrev; i++) s.push(undefined);"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new); open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - $D/Infographic.qml | timeout 130 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 "tar -C / -xf -; sync; systemctl stop tinydm; for i in \$(seq 1 20); do pgrep -x lomiri >/dev/null || break; sleep 1; done; sleep 2; systemctl start tinydm; sleep 15; systemctl is-active tinydm; md5sum /$D/Infographic.qml"; md5sum $D/Infographic.qml
```

**01:05** — Включаю экран блокировки и делаю снимок экрана виртуальным нажатием кнопок

```sh
cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/m52xq-shot.py <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/python3
# virtual keyboard: VolumeDown + Power = Lomiri screenshot (debug helper, not part of the image)
import fcntl, os, struct, time
UI_SET_EVBIT, UI_SET_KEYBIT, UI_DEV_CREATE, UI_DEV_DESTROY = 0x40045564, 0x40045565, 0x5501, 0x5502
EV_SYN, EV_KEY, KEY_VOLUMEDOWN, KEY_POWER = 0, 1, 114, 116
fd = os.open('/dev/uinput', os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
fcntl.ioctl(fd, UI_SET_EVBIT, EV_KEY)
for k in (KEY_VOLUMEDOWN, KEY_POWER):
	fcntl.ioctl(fd, UI_SET_KEYBIT, k)
name = b'm52xq-shot'.ljust(80, b'\0')
os.write(fd, name + struct.pack('HHHHi', 3, 1, 1, 1, 0) + b'\0' * (4 * 64 * 4))
fcntl.ioctl(fd, UI_DEV_CREATE)
time.sleep(1.5)
def ev(t, c, v):
	os.write(fd, struct.pack('llHHi', 0, 0, t, c, v))
ev(EV_KEY, KEY_VOLUMEDOWN, 1); ev(EV_SYN, 0, 0); time.sleep(0.05)
ev(EV_KEY, KEY_POWER, 1); ev(EV_SYN, 0, 0); time.sleep(0.3)
ev(EV_KEY, KEY_POWER, 0); ev(EV_SYN, 0, 0); time.sleep(0.05)
ev(EV_KEY, KEY_VOLUMEDOWN, 0); ev(EV_SYN, 0, 0)
time.sleep(0.5)
fcntl.ioctl(fd, UI_DEV_DESTROY)
EOF
cat /tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/m52xq-shot.py | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat > /tmp/m52xq-shot.py; before=$(ls -t /home/user/Pictures/Screenshots | head -1); su user -s /bin/sh -c "DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=unix:path=/run/user/10000/bus gdbus call --session -d org.gnome.ScreenSaver -o /org/gnome/ScreenSaver -m org.gnome.ScreenSaver.Lock" >/dev/null; sleep 4; python3 /tmp/m52xq-shot.py; sleep 3; after=$(ls -t /home/user/Pictures/Screenshots | head -1); echo "$before -> $after"' 2>&1
```

**01:06** — Правлю копию Infographics.qml: тёмный диск, оранжевый крупный круг со сдвигом наружу

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; G=usr/share/lomiri/Greeter; timeout 30 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat /usr/share/lomiri/Greeter/Infographics.qml' > $G/Infographics.qml.orig; python3 - $G/Infographics.qml.orig $G/Infographics.qml <<'EOF'
import sys;s=open(sys.argv[1]).read()
# 1) dark translucent disc under the dots (as on the Ubuntu Touch lock screen)
old="""        anchors.centerIn: parent

        Timer {
            id: circleChangeAnimTimer"""
new="""        anchors.centerIn: parent

        // m52xq: the dark translucent disc of the Ubuntu Touch lock screen
        Rectangle {
            anchors.centerIn: parent
            width: parent.width * 1.12
            height: width
            radius: width / 2
            color: "black"
            opacity: 0.22
        }

        Timer {
            id: circleChangeAnimTimer"""
assert s.count(old)==1; s=s.replace(old,new)
# 2) today's circle: a bit outside the ring, denser, Ubuntu orange
i=s.index('id: presentCircles')
seg=s[i:]
seg=seg.replace("                posOffset: 0.0\n","                posOffset: -0.25	// m52xq: centre a little outside the ring, as on Ubuntu Touch\n",1)
seg=seg.replace("                    property real circleOpacity: 0.3\n","                    property real circleOpacity: 0.65	// m52xq: was 0.3\n",1)
old_c="                                to: Gradient.threeColorByIndex(index, infographic.model.currentDay, whiteTheme)\n"
assert old_c in seg
seg=seg.replace(old_c,"                                to: \"#E95420\"	// m52xq: Ubuntu orange (was Gradient.threeColorByIndex)\n",1)
s=s[:i]+seg
open(sys.argv[2],'w').write(s)
EOF
diff $G/Infographics.qml.orig $G/Infographics.qml; D=usr/lib/lomiri/qml/LightDM/IntegratedLightDM; sed -i 's/f.push(i === today - 1 ? (n > 0 ? Math.min(1.0, 0.6 + 0.08 \* n) : 0.6) : undefined);/f.push(i === today - 1 ? 1.0 : undefined);	\/\/ today: the full size (the photo count is in the label)/' $D/Infographic.qml; grep -n "f.push" $D/Infographic.qml
```

**01:15** — Добавляю Wayland в окружение url-dispatcher и перезапускаю его

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; F=etc/systemd/user/lomiri-url-dispatcher.service; [ -f $F ] || cp ../../build/extra-003/$F $F; python3 - $F <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
if "QT_QPA_PLATFORM" not in s:
    s=s.replace("Type=dbus\n","# the apps it starts (System Settings from the shade) need Wayland; started before Lomiri exported QT_QPA_PLATFORM\n# into the session they came up with xcb and aborted (\"no Qt platform plugin could be initialized\")\nEnvironment=QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland WAYLAND_DISPLAY=wayland-0\nType=dbus\n")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
cat $F | tail -8; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - $F | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; su user -s /bin/sh -c "export XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/run/user/10000 DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS=unix:path=/run/user/10000/bus; systemctl --user daemon-reload; systemctl --user restart lomiri-url-dispatcher; systemctl --user is-active lomiri-url-dispatcher"; sync'
```

**11:48** — Исправляю кнопку перезагрузки в TWRP для Lomiri, кладу в в2 и на телефон

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files; mkdir -p usr/local/bin; cp /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/архив/.v42-zip-stage/mainline-extras/rootfs-files/usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery usr/local/bin/; python3 - usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
old='action=$(notify-send -i system-reboot -u critical -t 20000 \\\n'
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,'# Lomiri shows action buttons only on a "snap decision" (hint x-lomiri-snap-decisions): without it the notification had\n# no buttons and the script returned at once. Plasma ignores the hint.\naction=$(notify-send -i system-reboot -u critical -t 20000 -h string:x-lomiri-snap-decisions:true \\\n')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
sh -n usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery && sed -n 8,14p usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery; chmod 755 usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery; F=usr/local/share/applications/m52xq-reboot-recovery.desktop; mkdir -p usr/local/share/applications; timeout 30 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'cat /usr/share/applications/m52xq-reboot-recovery.desktop' > $F; sed -i 's|^Name=Reboot to Recovery|Name=Reboot to Recovery\nName[ru]=Перезагрузка в TWRP|' $F; cat $F; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery $F | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; md5sum /usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery; sync'; md5sum usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery
```

**11:49** — Пишу описание архива v2

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003; cat > archive-v2/ЧТО_НОВОГО.txt <<'EOF'
postmarketOS mainline + Lomiri (без Plasma) для Samsung Galaxy M52 5G (m52xq) — архив lomiri v2 (2026-10-25)

Ставится в раздел system, как v1: boot v241 + vendor_boot v238 + образ GPT (p1 pmOS_boot, p2 pmOS_root, растягивается на весь
system). Рутфс снят с телефона после v1 + правок дня; личные данные удалены, при установке адрес Bluetooth, прошивка модема
и реестр датчиков берутся с самого телефона.

Новое по сравнению с v1:
- Мастер первого запуска снова показывается (в v1 был уже пройден); страница Wi-Fi не пропускается.
- Экранная клавиатура во всех приложениях и в оболочке (кнопки питания/громкости больше не считаются клавиатурой, окно оболочки
  активируется), русская + английская раскладки, крупный глобус; в Morph (Qt 6) — модуль maliit для Qt 6.
- Русский язык и нормальный масштаб в приложениях (Настройки, Файлы, Калькулятор, Morph и др.).
- Экран блокировки как в Ubuntu Touch: фон Ubuntu Touch, часы, дата, круг с днями месяца, разблокировка свайпом.
- Кнопка питания: короткое нажатие — блокировка и выключение экрана / включение; долгое — меню выключения.
- Автоповорот экрана (iio-sensor-proxy + сигнал питания экрана для qtmir).
- Касания в приложениях (окна больше не поворачиваются отдельно от оболочки).
- Звук: служба m52xq-audio-reprobe поднимает звуковую карту после гонки старта ADSP.
- Модем: работает наш ModemManager r8 (две SIM); по умолчанию обе SIM, данные — SIM1, мобильный интернет выключен.
- Wi-Fi: агент паролей запущен (окно пароля появляется; пароль из окна пока теряется — подключение через nmcli).
- Шторка: «Параметры системы»/«Настройки звука/батареи» открывают Настройки, а не Firefox.
- Системные звуки (lomiri-sounds), обои Ubuntu Touch, кот на рабочем столе целиком в обеих ориентациях.
- Кнопка «Перезагрузка в TWRP» с подтверждением; редактор снимков экрана выключен (снимок просто сохраняется).

Не сделано: сон (suspend) при выключенном экране, заставка при загрузке (Plymouth без графики), окно пароля Wi-Fi.
Пароль user и root: <пароль>.
EOF
echo ok
```

```sh
set -e; M=/tmp/claude-1000/-home-sasha-Templates---------/21f124fa-4095-4276-a809-ddade279b463/scratchpad/m003; test -f $M/etc/os-release && test -d $M/usr/share/qcom; V=/home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в2/rootfs-files
sudo rm -rf $M/etc/m52xq/bt_addr $M/etc/m52xq/data-sim $M/etc/m52xq/sim-mode $M/etc/m52xq/lomiri-v1 $M/lib/firmware/modem.mdt $M/lib/firmware/modem.b?? $M/lib/firmware/modemr.jsn $M/lib/firmware/modem_pr $M/etc/NetworkManager/system-connections/<Wi-Fi>.nmconnection $M/etc/NetworkManager/system-connections/<Wi-Fi>.nmconnection "$M/var/lib/bluetooth/XX:XX:XX:XX:XX:XX" $M/etc/ssh/ssh_host_* $M/home/user/.ash_history $M/root/.ash_history $M/root/lomiri-wrapper.before-imdebug $M/root/v2-greeter-backup $M/var/lib/NetworkManager/seen-bssids $M/var/lib/NetworkManager/timestamps $M/var/lib/systemd/random-seed $M/home/user/.config/lomiri/wizard-has-run $M/home/user/.local/share/morph-browser $M/home/user/.config/morph-browser
sudo find $M/home/user/Pictures/Screenshots -mindepth 1 -delete
sudo find $M/usr/share/qcom/sm7325/Samsung/m52xq/sensors/registry -mindepth 1 -delete
sudo find $M/home/user/.cache -mindepth 1 -delete
sudo find $M/var/log/journal -mindepth 1 -delete
sudo truncate -s 0 $M/etc/machine-id
# the TWRP button (Lomiri confirmation) and its Russian name
sudo cp $V/usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery $M/usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery; sudo chmod 755 $M/usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery
sudo mkdir -p $M/usr/local/share/applications; sudo cp $V/usr/local/share/applications/m52xq-reboot-recovery.desktop $M/usr/local/share/applications/
# boot splash: plymouthd skips its graphical renderers when the kernel command line has no "splash"
D=etc/systemd/system/plymouth-start.service.d; mkdir -p $V/$D; cat > $V/$D/10-m52xq-splash.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003 v2: plymouthd loads its graphical renderers only when the kernel command line has "splash" (ours has
# not: "Creating non-graphical devices, since renderers are being explicitly skipped", a black screen at boot); hand it
# the real command line plus "splash"
[Service]
ExecStart=
ExecStart=/bin/sh -c 'exec /usr/sbin/plymouthd --mode=boot --pid-file=/run/plymouth/pid --attach-to-session --kernel-command-line="$(cat /proc/cmdline) splash"'
EOF
sudo mkdir -p $M/$D; sudo cp $V/$D/10-m52xq-splash.conf $M/$D/
sudo chown -R 0:0 $M/usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery $M/usr/local/share/applications/m52xq-reboot-recovery.desktop $M/$D
echo --- check; sudo ls $M/etc/m52xq $M/etc/NetworkManager/system-connections $M/var/lib/bluetooth $M/home/user/Pictures/Screenshots $M/root; sudo ls $M/lib/firmware | grep -c "^modem" || true; sudo ls $M/etc/ssh | grep -c key || true; sudo grep -rl "psk=<скрыто> $M/etc/NetworkManager || true; sudo ls $M/home/user/.config/lomiri; sudo grep -c snap-decisions $M/usr/local/bin/m52xq-reboot-recovery; sudo cat $M/$D/10-m52xq-splash.conf | tail -1; sudo grep -c "always-show-osk" $M/usr/share/glib-2.0/schemas/99-m52xq-lomiri.gschema.override
```

**12:25** — Добавляю ожидание ADSP для службы датчиков и запускаю датчики и iio-sensor-proxy

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; D=etc/systemd/system/hexagonrpcd-adsp-sensorspd.service.d; mkdir -p $D; cat > $D/20-m52xq-wait-adsp.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003 v3: the unit was skipped at boot ("start condition unmet": ConditionPathExists=/dev/fastrpc-adsp) because
# the ADSP comes up later (~51 s on the v2 first boot), so there were no sensors: iio-sensor-proxy timed out and the
# screen never rotated. Wait for the device instead (the SDSP condition is not needed on this phone).
[Unit]
ConditionPathExists=
After=m52xq-adsp-start.service

[Service]
ExecStartPre=/bin/sh -c 'for i in $(seq 120); do [ -e /dev/fastrpc-adsp ] && exit 0; sleep 1; done; exit 1'
TimeoutStartSec=150
EOF
cat $D/20-m52xq-wait-adsp.conf | tail -6; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - $D | timeout 120 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; systemctl daemon-reload; systemctl start hexagonrpcd-adsp-sensorspd; sleep 3; systemctl is-active hexagonrpcd-adsp-sensorspd; systemctl restart iio-sensor-proxy; sleep 5; systemctl is-active iio-sensor-proxy; busctl --system get-property net.hadess.SensorProxy /net/hadess/SensorProxy net.hadess.SensorProxy HasAccelerometer AccelerometerOrientation; sleep 2; journalctl -u m52xq-powerkey -b --no-pager | tail -2 | cut -c40-140; sync' 2>&1
```

**12:50** — Переписываю скрипт заставки с пересчётом раскладки и включаю журнал Plymouth на загрузку

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; T=usr/share/plymouth/themes/m52xq-ubuntu-touch; mkdir -p $T; cp -a ../../в2/rootfs-files/$T/. $T/; cat > $T/m52xq-ubuntu-touch.script <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003: Ubuntu Touch boot splash (background: the Ubuntu Touch default, logo and dots: build/splash/make-splash.py)
# v3: the layout is (re)done in the refresh callback as soon as the screen size is known: at boot the script runs before
# the DRM head is up (Window.GetWidth() 0), the logo and the dots were scaled to nothing and only the background remained.

bg_src = Image("background.png");
logo_src = Image("logo.png");
dot_on = Image("dot-on.png");
dot_off = Image("dot-off.png");

bg_sprite = Sprite();
bg_sprite.SetZ(-100);
logo_sprite = Sprite();
for (i = 0; i < 5; i++)
    dot[i] = Sprite();
laid_w = 0;
laid_h = 0;

fun layout() {
    W = Window.GetWidth();
    H = Window.GetHeight();
    if (W <= 0 || H <= 0)
        return 0;
    if (W == laid_w && H == laid_h)
        return 1;
    laid_w = W;
    laid_h = H;
    bg_sprite.SetImage(bg_src.Scale(W, H));
    bg_sprite.SetPosition(0, 0, -100);
    s = (W * 0.62) / logo_src.GetWidth();
    logo = logo_src.Scale(logo_src.GetWidth() * s, logo_src.GetHeight() * s);
    logo_sprite.SetImage(logo);
    logo_y = H / 2 - logo.GetHeight() / 2;
    logo_sprite.SetPosition(W / 2 - logo.GetWidth() / 2, logo_y, 0);
    step = W * 0.045;
    dots_y = logo_y + logo.GetHeight() + H * 0.03;
    for (i = 0; i < 5; i++)
        dot[i].SetPosition(W / 2 + (i - 2) * step - dot_off.GetWidth() / 2, dots_y, 1);
    return 1;
}

# the bright dot runs left to right, one position every 0.4 s (refresh is 50 Hz)
frame = 0;
fun refresh_callback() {
    if (!layout())
        return;
    on = Math.Int(frame / 20) % 5;
    for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        if (i == on)
            dot[i].SetImage(dot_on);
        else
            dot[i].SetImage(dot_off);
    }
    frame++;
}
Plymouth.SetRefreshFunction(refresh_callback);

# messages (fsck, shutdown) near the bottom
msg_sprite = Sprite();
fun message_callback(text) {
# … ещё 6 строк
```

**12:56** — Исправляю порядок подписки на акселерометр в m52xq-powerkey и проверяю

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; mkdir -p usr/local/sbin; cp ../../в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey usr/local/sbin/; python3 - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
old="""	def claim(*_):
		try:
			bus.call_sync(proxy, '/net/hadess/SensorProxy', proxy, 'ClaimAccelerometer', None, None,
				Gio.DBusCallFlags.NONE, 5000, None)
			log('accelerometer claimed')
		except GLib.Error as e:
			log('ClaimAccelerometer: ' + e.message)"""
new="""	# v3: claim only once the proxy has found the accelerometer: a claim that comes in right when the proxy appears (before
	# its SSC accelerometer is probed) is counted but never enables the sensor, and every later claim (Lomiri,
	# monitor-sensor) then sees the count above 0: the orientation stayed "undefined" (v2 boot, 25.10)
	def has_accel():
		try:
			v = bus.call_sync(proxy, '/net/hadess/SensorProxy', 'org.freedesktop.DBus.Properties', 'Get',
				GLib.Variant('(ss)', (proxy, 'HasAccelerometer')), None, Gio.DBusCallFlags.NONE, 2000, None)
			return v.unpack()[0]
		except GLib.Error:
			return False

	def claim(*_):
		tries = [0]

		def attempt():
			tries[0] += 1
			if not has_accel():
				return tries[0] < 120	# keep trying every 0.5 s for a minute
			try:
				bus.call_sync(proxy, '/net/hadess/SensorProxy', proxy, 'ClaimAccelerometer', None, None,
					Gio.DBusCallFlags.NONE, 5000, None)
				log('accelerometer claimed')
			except GLib.Error as e:
				log('ClaimAccelerometer: ' + e.message)
			return False
		GLib.timeout_add(500, attempt)"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new); open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
python3 -m py_compile usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey && echo ok; rm -rf usr/local/sbin/__pycache__; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey | timeout 120 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@172.16.42.1 'tar -C / -xf -; chmod 755 /usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey; systemctl stop m52xq-powerkey; systemctl restart iio-sensor-proxy; systemctl start m52xq-powerkey; for i in $(seq 1 20); do o=$(busctl --system get-property net.hadess.SensorProxy /net/hadess/SensorProxy net.hadess.SensorProxy AccelerometerOrientation 2>/dev/null); [ -n "$o" ] && [ "$o" != "s \"undefined\"" ] && break; sleep 2; done; echo "$o"; journalctl -u m52xq-powerkey -b --no-pager | tail -3 | cut -c40-140; sync'; md5sum usr/local/sbin/m52xq-powerkey
```

**13:25** — Добавляю ожидание iio-sensor-proxy перед запуском Lomiri и записываю в README и план

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; D=etc/systemd/system/tinydm.service.d; mkdir -p $D; cat > $D/20-m52xq-after-sensors.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003 v3: Lomiri must start after iio-sensor-proxy. qtmir's orientation sensor (QtSensors iio-sensor-proxy
# backend in the Mir thread, which gets no D-Bus signals) notes at start that the proxy is not there and never sees it
# appear: no rotation at all when Lomiri came up at ~15 s and the proxy at ~37 s (it waits for SSC accelerometer data).
# The proxy gives up waiting after 60 s and starts anyway, so this delays Lomiri by at most about a minute.
[Unit]
Wants=iio-sensor-proxy.service
After=iio-sensor-proxy.service
EOF
cat $D/20-m52xq-after-sensors.conf | tail -3; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - $D | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@192.168.1.122 'tar -C / -xf -; systemctl daemon-reload; systemctl show tinydm -p After | tr " " "\n" | grep iio; sync'; cd ..; echo "11. (сделано, проверить при загрузке) tinydm After/Wants=iio-sensor-proxy (drop-in tinydm.service.d/20-m52xq-after-sensors.conf): без этого qtmir не видит датчик, если Lomiri стартовала раньше proxy." >> ЧТО_ДЕЛАТЬ.txt; echo "* Автоповорот после загрузки: qtmir (Qt iio-backend в потоке Mir без D-Bus-сигналов) запоминает «proxy нет», если Lomiri стартовала раньше iio-sensor-proxy (15 с против 37 с); перезапуск Lomiri при живом proxy → поворот есть (подтверждено). v3: drop-in tinydm.service.d/20-m52xq-after-sensors.conf (Wants/After=iio-sensor-proxy) — Lomiri позже на ~20 с; проверить при следующей загрузке." >> ../../README.md
```

**13:36** — Пишу передачу экрана от заставки к Lomiri через tinydm и проверяю юниты

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; cat > etc/systemd/system/tinydm.service.d/30-m52xq-splash-handoff.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003 v3: hand the screen from the boot splash to Lomiri without the text console in between. Right before
# the session starts: stop the systemd status lines on the console (SIGRTMIN+21; the boot messages before stay as they
# are) and quit Plymouth keeping its last frame, which stays until Mir draws its first one. "+" = as root (User=10000).
[Service]
ExecStartPre=-+/bin/kill -s RTMIN+21 1
ExecStartPre=-+/usr/bin/plymouth quit --retain-splash
EOF
cat > etc/systemd/system/plymouth-quit.service.d/20-m52xq-until-lomiri.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003 v3: Plymouth is quit by tinydm right before Lomiri (tinydm.service.d/30-m52xq-splash-handoff.conf);
# this unit only stays as a fallback after it
[Unit]
After=iio-sensor-proxy.service tinydm.service
EOF
cat > etc/systemd/system/plymouth-quit-wait.service.d/20-m52xq-until-lomiri.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003 v3: tinydm (After=plymouth-quit-wait) must not wait for Plymouth to exit: tinydm quits it itself
[Service]
ExecStart=
ExecStart=/bin/true
EOF
```

**13:36** — Выношу передачу экрана в скрипт m52xq-splash-handoff и ставлю на телефон

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; cat > usr/local/sbin/m52xq-splash-handoff <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# M52XQ (убунтутач24/003 v3): run by tinydm (as root) right before the Lomiri session: no systemd status lines on the
# console from now on (SIGRTMIN+21 to PID 1, the SIGRTMIN of the musl libc systemd is built with: 35+21 = 56; the
# boot messages before stay as they are), then quit Plymouth keeping its last frame until Mir draws its first one.
python3 -c 'import os, signal; os.kill(1, signal.SIGRTMIN + 21)'
plymouth quit --retain-splash
exit 0
EOF
chmod 755 usr/local/sbin/m52xq-splash-handoff; sh -n usr/local/sbin/m52xq-splash-handoff && echo ok; cat > etc/systemd/system/tinydm.service.d/30-m52xq-splash-handoff.conf <<'EOF'
# убунтутач24/003 v3: hand the screen from the boot splash to Lomiri without the text console in between
# (usr/local/sbin/m52xq-splash-handoff; "+" = as root, the unit runs as User=10000)
[Service]
ExecStartPre=-+/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-splash-handoff
EOF
tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-splash-handoff etc/systemd/system/tinydm.service.d/30-m52xq-splash-handoff.conf | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@192.168.1.122 'tar -C / -xf -; systemctl daemon-reload; systemctl show tinydm -p ExecStartPre | grep -o "argv\[\]=[^;]*"; sync'; cd ../..; echo "* Перед рабочим столом 2 с логов: строки статуса systemd в консоли, пока Mir рисует первый кадр. v3: tinydm ExecStartPre=+/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-splash-handoff (SIGRTMIN+21 → PID 1 = статус systemd выкл., plymouth quit --retain-splash), plymouth-quit After=tinydm (запасной), plymouth-quit-wait ExecStart=/bin/true. Логи до заставки не тронуты." >> ../README.md; echo "13. (проверить при загрузке) передача экрана заставка→Lomiri: m52xq-splash-handoff в tinydm ExecStartPre." >> в3/ЧТО_ДЕЛАТЬ.txt
```

**13:44** — Обновляю службу восстановления звука на оба драйвера, в в3 и на телефоне

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; mkdir -p usr/local/sbin; cp ../../в2/rootfs-files/usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe usr/local/sbin/; cat > usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# M52XQ: the sound card after a lost ADSP start race. The LPASS drivers that vote through the ADSP AFE are sometimes probed
# right after its APR service, before the audio PD is up: "q6afe cmd 0x100f4 returned error 0x16", "AFE failed to vote
# (3)", probe -110, and a driver never tries again. Then rx/tx macro, SoundWire and the card wait forever ("no
# soundcards"). Which drivers lose it varies: va_macro (3370000.codec) on one boot, also the LPASS LPI pin controller
# (33c0000.lpass-lpi-pinctrl) on another (25.10, v3). Retry the probe of the ones without a driver until the card is there.
DEVS="33c0000.lpass-lpi-pinctrl 3370000.codec"
for i in $(seq 40); do
	grep -q '^ *[0-9]' /proc/asound/cards 2>/dev/null && exit 0
	for d in $DEVS; do
		[ -e /sys/bus/platform/devices/$d ] || continue
		[ -e /sys/bus/platform/devices/$d/driver ] && continue
		echo $d > /sys/bus/platform/drivers_probe 2>/dev/null
		[ -e /sys/bus/platform/devices/$d/driver ] && echo "m52xq-audio-reprobe: $d bound (try $i)"
	done
	sleep 3
done
echo "m52xq-audio-reprobe: still no sound card" >&2
exit 1
EOF
chmod 755 usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe; sh -n usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe && echo ok; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe | timeout 60 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@192.168.1.122 'tar -C / -xf -; md5sum /usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe; sync'; md5sum usr/local/sbin/m52xq-audio-reprobe; cd ../../..; echo "* Звук пропал (загрузка 25.10 13:40): гонка ADSP задела и va_macro, и LPASS LPI pinctrl (33c0000.lpass-lpi-pinctrl); reprobe привязал только va_macro → rx/tx macro ждали pinctrl, карта не появилась. Ручной drivers_probe pinctrl → карта есть. v3: m52xq-audio-reprobe повторяет оба (в3, на телефоне)." >> README.md
```

```sh
EOF
```

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/build-indicator-bluetooth/ayatana-indicator-bluetooth-26.6.1/src; cp phone.vala phone.vala.orig; python3 - phone.vala <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""class Phone: Profile
{
  SimpleActionGroup action_group;
""","""class Phone: Profile
{
  SimpleActionGroup action_group;
  // m52xq (убунтутач24/003 v3): the paired (trusted) devices with a connect switch, as on the desktop menu; upstream the
  // phone menu had only the Bluetooth switch and the settings link
  Menu device_section;
  HashTable<uint,SimpleAction> connect_actions;
  uint idle_rebuild_id = 0;
""",1)
s=s.replace("""    var section = new Menu ();
    section.append_item (create_enabled_menuitem ());
    section.append (_("Bluetooth settings…"),
                    "indicator.phone-show-settings::bluetooth");
    menu.append_section (null, section);
""","""    connect_actions = new HashTable<uint,SimpleAction>(direct_hash, direct_equal);

    var section = new Menu ();
    section.append_item (create_enabled_menuitem ());
    menu.append_section (null, section);

    device_section = new Menu ();
    rebuild_device_section ();
    menu.append_section (null, device_section);

    section = new Menu ();
    section.append (_("Bluetooth settings…"),
                    "indicator.phone-show-settings::bluetooth");
    menu.append_section (null, section);

    bluetooth.devices_changed.connect (() => {
      if (idle_rebuild_id == 0)
```

**14:15** — Добавляю запуск служб звонков и SMS в обёртку и перезапускаю Lomiri

```sh
cd /home/sasha/Templates/майнлайн/убунтутач24/003/в3/rootfs-files; W=usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/lomiri; python3 - $W <<'EOF'
import sys;p=sys.argv[1];s=open(p).read()
old="    # the Wi-Fi password dialog"
new="""    # calls and SMS (plasma-dialer, spacebar): their daemons come with XDG autostart files, which nothing runs here
    for d in modem-daemon kde-telephony-daemon spacebar-daemon; do
        [ -x /usr/lib/libexec/$d ] && ! pgrep -u "$(id -u)" -x $d >/dev/null && /usr/lib/libexec/$d &
    done
    # the Wi-Fi password dialog"""
assert s.count(old)==1; s=s.replace(old,new,1); open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
sh -n $W && grep -n -A3 "calls and SMS" $W; tar --owner=0 --group=0 -cf - $W | timeout 120 sshpass -p <пароль> ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR root@192.168.1.122 'tar -C / -xf -; chmod 755 /usr/local/lib/m52xq/lomiri-bin/lomiri; sync; systemctl stop tinydm; for i in $(seq 1 20); do pgrep -x lomiri >/dev/null || break; sleep 1; done; sleep 2; systemctl start tinydm; sleep 25; systemctl is-active tinydm; pgrep -a -f "modem-daemon|kde-telephony-daemon|spacebar-daemon" | grep -v pgrep | cut -c1-80'
```
